- **POL-50** COST-ARC FUEL / MILEAGE / AU-FUEL-INCLUSION rulings (Gaurav, 2026-07-28). **FUEL (cost side, per FLOW-11/17):** fuel we PAY hosts = `fuel_inclusion` → **5023** (SG-only product; **AU has NO fuel-inclusion**). Fuel REFUNDED to guests = `fuel_refund` → **4029 Fuel Refund** (the contra already built on the revenue side; both markets). `fuel_charge` (collected from host) = contra to host-payable, NOT a 5xxx cost. **DROP `view_AU_a_cost_fuel_inclusion`** — AU fuel-inclusion doesn't exist; creating it was wrong (resolves the 5023-AU open item). **EXCESS MILEAGE:** forget `mileage_package` for now — SG reuses existing `view_SG_a_host_incidentals_excess_mileage_new` (excess_mileage only, matching AU); **DROP held `view_SG_a_cost_excess_mileage`** (resolves 5024-SG). **LATE RETURN (5044):** must be its OWN clean cost view (currently buried as a column in the multi-purpose accrual view) — population decision pending (accrual-scoped 7,763 vs all-payouts 10,011). *(Gaurav, 2026-07-28)*
- **POL-51** AU FUEL REFUND — CHARGE-HOST=REVENUE, PAY-GUEST=COST; NO 2120 NETTING (Gaurav, 2026-07-28). **CORRECTS FLOW-11 stream 3 and the earlier Atlas/orchestrator "fuel_charge nets to 2120" (POL-50/DQ) — that was WRONG.** AU-ONLY flow (SG has NO fuel refund). Economics are reversed vs inclusion/shortage: we **PAY the guest** (a **COST**) and **CHARGE the host** (**REVENUE**, "fuel charged to host"). In `au_payout_entries` the host-charge appears as a **NEGATIVE fuel amount**. **RULING: a negative fuel payout entry = fuel charged to host = REVENUE — it must NOT net off against 2120 host payables; it appears as revenue.** The equivalent fuel-refund-to-guest = COST. For ALL OTHER fuel (inclusion + shortage, BOTH markets): **charge guest = REVENUE (4023 Fuel Charge), pay host = COST (5023 Cost of Fuel)** — AU & SG accounts must be SYMMETRIC. Underlying principle across all fuel: **fuel we CHARGE anyone = revenue; fuel we PAY anyone = cost** (counterparty-agnostic). Account assignment DECIDED (Gaurav — dedicated lines): charge-host → **NEW 4032 Incidentals Revenue – Fuel Charged to Host** (REVENUE/CREDIT); refund-guest → **NEW 5037 Cost of Fuel Refund to Guest** (COST_OF_SALES/DEBIT); guest fuel charge (inclusion/shortage) → **4023**; host fuel pay (inclusion/shortage) → **5023**. **4029 "Fuel Refund" (contra-revenue, POL-43) is now REDUNDANT → retire** (flagged, pending retire approval; still ACTIVE, unwired). Data sources (to build, propose-only): 4032 ← AU negative fuel payout entries (~A$32,314 H1); 5037 ← AU fuel refund to guest (~A$15,906 H1). *(Gaurav, 2026-07-28)*
- **DQ-28** DATA-SOURCING ARCHITECTURE — confirmed (Gaurav Q + orchestrator-verified, 2026-07-28). Three sources, cleanly separated: **(1) HOST payouts (5xxx cost) ← `{market}_payout_entries`** — AU 100%; SG all LIVE `_new` views. **(2) GUEST cash (charges/refunds/settlements/deposits, the `_c_` views) ← `{market}_stripe_balance_transactions`** — both markets 100%. **(3) GUEST invoiced revenue (4xxx accrual — incidentals/subscription) ← `{market}_stripe_invoices`.** CAVEAT: SG has **7 DEAD OLD host views** (no `_new` suffix: host_flexplus_payout, host_incidentals_damage/_excess_mileage/_fuel, host_misc_payout, host_sticker_payout, host_trip_earnings_accrual) that still read `sg_stripe_balance_transactions` (pre-migration generation, NULL for 2026 per DQ-22) → **retire candidates**. Verified by extracting FROM-clauses of all `_a_host_`/`_c_` view definitions. *(Gaurav Q, 2026-07-28)*
- **POL-52** MISC_PAYOUT → CLASSIFY BY DESCRIPTION (Gaurav, 2026-07-28). `misc_payout` (payout_entries; SG S$1.74M / AU S$722k) is a dumping ground → classify by the `description` field (mirror the incidentals classifier + a lookup). Carve-outs: **sticker→5041, superhost→5040, damage/total-loss/AFCA/settlement→5021, towing→5033, downtime→5043, parking→5060**; genuine residual → **5042 Misc** (expect large ~S$1.6M blank/ambiguous). Both markets. Resolves DQ-26/Q6 — sticker & superhost streams MIGRATED into misc_payout (that's why the old code-3/code-4 dedicated views went dead). *(Gaurav, 2026-07-28)*
- **DQ-29** PAYOUT-VIEW FILTER SET — three filters, applied INCONSISTENTLY (orchestrator-verified from view DDL, 2026-07-28). The host-payout views layer up to three filters on `{market}_payout_entries`: **(1) `finance_status ≠ 'Under Review'`** (manual finance-hold gate) — on EVERY payout view; **(2) `payoutStatus ≠ 'cancelled'`** — on trip-earnings/late-return/P2P-RMS-split views, **but MISSING on the incidentals payout views** (damage/tolls/cleaning/etc. only filter finance_status) ⚠️; **(3) `tripStatus NOT IN ('payment-failed','cancelled-refundable','unverified','payment-pending')` + non-null `accrual_month`** — ONLY on `view_{mkt}_a_host_trip_earnings_accrual_new` (the eligibility gate). **Consequence:** late-return (5044) has two numbers ONLY because the accrual view adds filter (3): all-payouts S$10,010.61 vs accrual-scoped S$7,763.40 (Δ S$2,247 = late_return payouts on the 4 excluded tripStatuses). Proposed policy (pending Gaurav): standardise ALL cost views to baseline (1)+(2), DROP the tripStatus eligibility gate for cost-truth (5044→10,011), and ADD the missing payoutStatus filter to the incidentals payout views. *(orchestrator-verified, 2026-07-28)*
- **DQ-30** PAYOUT STATUS UNIVERSE — the `!= cancelled` filter is a NO-OP; tripStatus is the real gate (orchestrator-verified H1, 2026-07-28). **payoutStatus** has NO 'cancelled' value — only `paid` (AU 17,090/$1.34M · SG 19,691/S$2.0M), `created` = accrued-but-unpaid (AU 4,839/$565k · SG 31,420/**S$4.19M**), `ready` (AU 16/$206). So every `payoutStatus != 'cancelled'` filter does nothing; cancellations live in **tripStatus**. **tripStatus H1:** SG `cancelled-refundable` = **S$3.47M (BIGGER than completed S$1.98M!)** · AU cancelled-refundable $237k. Since most payout views apply NO tripStatus filter, they carry that cancelled-refundable as cost (esp SG). The trip-earnings view's 4-status exclusion (`payment-failed,cancelled-refundable,unverified,payment-pending`) is INCOMPLETE — misses **plain `cancelled`** (AU $8.4k/SG S$55k), **`error`** (AU $28.8k), **null/blank/N-A** (AU ~$91k incl a NEGATIVE −$16.5k), and doesn't address **`ongoing`/`upcoming`** (not-yet-completed = arguably unearned; AU $84k/SG $38k). `enquiry`=$0 harmless. **Decision pending:** the definitive tripStatus exclusion set to standardise across all payout views. *(orchestrator-verified, 2026-07-28)*
- **POL-53** TRIPSTATUS FILTER = EXCLUSION PRINCIPLE (Gaurav, 2026-07-28). All payout accrual views filter `tripStatus` by **EXCLUSION, not inclusion** (default-INCLUDE; exclude only the named set — robust to future/unknown statuses). **Exclude set: `cancelled-refundable`, `payment-failed`, `unverified`, `error`, `enquiry`.** Everything else INCLUDED (completed, returned, ongoing, upcoming, cancelled(plain), payment-pending, N/A, blank, null). Standardise across ALL AU+SG payout accrual views (fixes DQ-29 inconsistency — most views had NO tripStatus filter). Also per DQ-30 the `payoutStatus != 'cancelled'` filter is a NO-OP (no such value) → drop/replace it; `created`+`paid`+`ready` all accrue. **RESOLVED (Gaurav):** plain `cancelled` = **NON-refundable** (guest was still charged, host still earns) → **INCLUDED**; `cancelled-refundable` = refunded (no real charge) → **EXCLUDED**. The two statuses are semantically different — that's the whole reason only the refundable one is excluded. Final exclude set is exactly: cancelled-refundable · payment-failed · unverified · error · enquiry. *(Gaurav, 2026-07-28)*
- **DQ-31** POL-53 FILTER IMPACT — SG host-payout restates −S$4.07M (Atlas, 2026-07-28). Applying the POL-53 tripStatus exclusion across all payout views removes **phantom accrual**: **SG Host Payout Regular (5000/5001): S$6.00M → S$1.94M (−S$4.07M)** = S$3.49M `cancelled-refundable` (ALL `payoutStatus='created'` — accrued-but-NEVER-paid) + S$0.58M payment-failed/unverified. **AU Regular: ~A$1.23M → A$721k (−A$300k)**, mostly cancelled-refundable on legacy `trip` type. Other accounts move marginally. 39 of 41 payout views need the filter added (only the 2 trip_earnings views had any tripStatus filter — and they use a DIVERGENT set: `payment-pending`, no `error`/`enquiry` → reconcile to canonical). `view_AU_intermediate_payout_transactions` = classify before applying (may be a feeder). **CROSS-ARC FLAG:** revenue views MUST exclude the same cancelled-refundable trips (or confirm Stripe refunds net them) — else cutting cost while keeping revenue overstates profit. *(Atlas, 2026-07-28)*
- **DQ-32** misc_payout description SOURCE + AU/SG content divergence (Gaurav Q, 2026-07-28). Descriptions come from the SINGLE `description` field (Nullable String) on `{market}_payout_entries` — identical structure both markets, no separate comment/notes/metadata. Format is ticket-prefixed: `Ticket [no/number/link]: <id> - Ref: <ref> - <REASON>`; the classifiable type = the trailing reason (keyword-searchable). **CONTENT DIFFERS by market — classify separately, do NOT conflate:** **AU** = AFCA complaints/compensation (Australia-only dispute body), trip/distance CORRECTIONS ("correct/missing distance", "rebilling", "gap hours", "additional days" → host trip-payout fixes, 5000/1), goodwill, operational (towing/key/storage). **SG** = "Loss of use" (DOMINANT → 5021-adjacent), "Correction for long term discount" (SG-only → 5050), goodwill, downtime, operational. So misc classifier needs MARKET-SPECIFIC keyword maps. *(Gaurav Q, 2026-07-28)*
- **FLOW-23** RMS REGULAR host-payout economics — 4 elements; 5001 was WRONG (Gaurav, 2026-07-28). Regular rentals only (NOT Flex+). REVENUE side clean (P2P/RMS split by connect account, Stripe). PAYOUT/COST side has **4 elements**: **(1)** `payout_entries` records the host-earnings PAYABLE against our OWN connect account for RMS trips (mechanism='rms' duration/distance/trip — like any connect account); **(2)** that payable is PAID to our own connect account = cash movement platform→our-connect-account, captured in **host_transfers** (`view_{mkt}_c_host_transfers`) which we **NET OFF** → so **(1)+(2) are INTERNAL and cancel — NOT a real cost**; **(3)** the REAL RMS cost = separate payout to actual car owners via THREE channels: (a) `payoutType='rms'` (SG S$7,286 H1; **AU has NONE**), (b) some via `misc_payout` (separable by description), (c) **DIRECT BANK transfers** to many RMS owners (outside payout_entries — CBA/OCBC) → AU RMS owner cost is essentially ALL bank; **(4)** smaller: cash transfers to **verification/deposit** accounts (not payout_entries) — confirm captured. **CONSEQUENCE:** Atlas's 5001 wrongly booked the INTERNAL element-1 payables (SG S$48,799 / AU A$13,605) as cost. **5001 (RMS cost) must = element (3) only.** Investigation launched (Hermes) on host-cash-payout mechanics (transfer vs payout) + verification/deposit capture. *(Gaurav, 2026-07-28)*
- **FLOW-24** RMS COST — Hermes verification + 2 gaps (Hermes, 2026-07-28). **Host cash-out mechanics:** host EARNINGS cash-out = `view_{mkt}_c_host_transfers` (Stripe `reporting_category='transfer'`, platform→connect); `view_{mkt}_c_stripe_payouts` = connect→bank sweep (small: SG 36/−S$567k, AU 12/−A$205k — this is our managed connect accts draining to ENT-7 banks = funds element 2). No double-count (different reporting_category). **CAVEAT:** host_transfers NETS 4 categories incl. `platform_earning` +S$955,388 (OUR fee income) → the net (SG −S$2.05M) conflates host cash-out with platform fee retention; pure transfer-out leg = −S$3.39M SG. Don't use host_transfers as "pure host cash." **Elements 1+2 CONFIRMED net** (element 1 reproduced exact SG S$48,799/AU A$13,605; matching transfer to our connect accts exists, settles ENT-7 banks) — but net on MATCHED SCOPE, not raw (element 2 scoped wider). **Element 3 (REAL RMS cost) = SG S$7,285.71 (payoutType='rms') + AU A$220,963 DIRECT-BANK owners** (Bombora −110k · CDC Fleet −103k · Norman −5k · Abwin −3k; CP-18; paid via Wise ex CBA-7311; RECONCILED). **misc_payout channel is EMPTY — corrects FLOW-23** (no RMS hides there). **GAP-1 (actionable):** the 30 AU owner txns have counterparty but `categorization_type=NULL`, NO rule → unrouted to 5001; existing 5001 rules are SG-only + mostly INACTIVE; no active rule for Bombora/CDC (A$212k). **GAP-2:** element-4 internal transfers INTO deposit/verification connect accts (AU deposit acct A$169,921/9,092 txns; SG verification S$14,335) are NOT captured by any `_c_` view (which only see guest charge/refund side) — confirm netting or orphaned. *(Hermes, 2026-07-28)*
- **FLOW-25** HOST PAYOUT CASH MECHANISM — confirmed (Gaurav + verified, 2026-07-28). **(1) DURING the month:** guest pays → a per-trip Stripe **transfer** to the host connect account + **`platform_earning`** (our app fee) at payment time (~1:1 with charges: SG 21,258 charges / 21,157 transfers / 19,826 platform_earnings H1). This accumulates in the host's connect account. **(2) FIRST WEEK of next month:** host payables (`payout_entries`, 98% `payoutDate` in week1 — SG S$2.09M vs S$76k rest) are settled: we **TOP UP** the gap between the payable and what's already accumulated in the connect account (week1 transfers = SG S$1.04M; per-trip transfers days 8-31 = S$2.34M), then the connect account pays out to the host's bank. Example: host earns S$2,000 (payable), has ~S$1,500 accumulated → we top up S$500 → payout to bank. **(3) The `reporting_category='payout'` rows (SG 36 / −S$567k) are NOT host payouts — they are OUR PLATFORM → OUR bank sweeps** (our fee income/managed balance to OCBC). **BOOKING CONSEQUENCE:** host COST = the `payout_entries` PAYABLE (accrual — what Atlas's cost views already use ✅); transfers (per-trip + week1 top-up) = the CASH DELIVERY of it (settlement, not a 2nd cost); `platform_earning` (S$955k) = OUR fee income (revenue, keep OUT of host cost); the `payout` sweep = balance-sheet cash movement. Do NOT read `host_transfers` net (−S$2.05M) as host cost — it conflates platform_earning + the sweep. *(corrects orchestrator's earlier "month-end doesn't create transfers" — first-week top-ups DO exist; Gaurav 2026-07-28)*
- **POL-54** PLATFORM_EARNING = OUR COMMISSION/REVENUE; host cost booked NET from the payable (Gaurav + verified, 2026-07-28). **Reconciliation SG H1:** net transfer (`transfer` − `platform_earning`, incl. reversals/fee-refunds) = **−S$2,053,166** ≈ total `payout_entries` payable (all types, POL-53 filter) **S$2,126,784** = 97% match (~S$73k timing/RMS-internal gap). CONFIRMS: (1) the `payout_entries` **payable = NET host cost** (net of our commission) → **Atlas's cost views book host cost correctly from the payable**; (2) **`platform_earning` (SG S$955k H1) = OUR commission / application-fee = REVENUE (the take-rate), NOT a host-cost line** — it's the margin between gross transfer and net payout; (3) book host cost from the **PAYABLE** (accrual, per-type → the 5xxx breakdown); the **net-transfer is a CASH CONTROL** that reconciles to it. **Do NOT** book gross transfer as cost; **do NOT** net platform_earning into cost (payable is already net). platform_earning books as commission revenue (or emerges as GBV−host-payout margin). Closes Stream-1 (host cash-out mechanics). *(Gaurav, 2026-07-28)*
- **POL-55** RMS INTERNAL PAYOUTS → CONNECT-ACCOUNT CLEARING (not cost) (Gaurav, 2026-07-28). The internal RMS-mechanism payouts to our OWN connect accounts (FLOW-23 element 1; SG S$48,799 / AU A$13,605 H1) are NOT a cost → book them as **cash transfers to a per-market Connect-Account Clearing account** (balance sheet; ONE per market SG/AU, tied to our RMS connect accounts OCBC-1001 / CBA-7311, ENT-7). The cash flowing BACK from the connect account to our platform/bank is captured by the existing **"$1000 rule"** (connect-account auto-match at GROSS level, NOT per-transaction) → **nets off** against the clearing account. Net effect: internal loop, ~zero P&L. **Real RMS cost (FLOW-23 element 3: `rms` payoutType + AU direct-bank owners) → 5001 separately.** Verification/deposit connect accounts = separate stream (later, POL-TBD). *(Gaurav, 2026-07-28)*
- **FLOW-26** "$1000 RULE" = a THRESHOLD PAIR (Atlas, 2026-07-28). The connect-account settlement auto-match is two rules on SG Stripe→OCBC settlements, both `description_value='CSDB STRIPE PAYMENTS'`, INCOMING, category `INTERNAL_TRANSFER` (no P&L, no contra): **rule 30** (amount `>999.99` → bank 18 Stripe-SG → bank 19 OCBC-3001, operating) + **rule 214** (amount `<1000` → banks[1,18] → bank 20 Fleet = the **Fleet/RMS gross earnings landing** — the connect-account cash-back that nets against 2130 clearing per POL-55). Banks: **1=OCBC-1001, 18=Stripe-SG-platform, 19=OCBC-3001, 20=Fleet**. `finance_categorization_rules` matches via `description_value`/`amount_value`/`counterparty_value` + `*_operator` fields (**NO `pattern` column**). AU RMS-owner rules: only **Abwin** exists (id 40, →5001, INACTIVE); **Bombora/CDC/Norman MISSING** (A$212k unrouted). *(Atlas, 2026-07-28)*
- **POL-56** "HOST TRIP EARNINGS" naming + RMS 5001 composition (Gaurav, 2026-07-28). **RENAME 5000-5003 accounts + events "Host Payouts" → "Host Trip Earnings"** — "payout" collides with the CASH payout; "trip earnings" = what the host earns (our cost/payable). **5001 RMS Host Trip Earnings = (1) `payoutType='rms'` (manual monthly owner pays) + (2) manual BANK payments to RMS owners (Bombora/CDC/Norman/Abwin, via categorization rules → 5001) + (3) `misc_payout` WHERE the DESCRIPTION explicitly says RMS** — **description-only, NEVER infer by hostId (Gaurav, firm); currently ~0 in H1 (Hermes found no rms/caretaker keywords in misc), the keyword catches future ones.** **EXCLUDED from 5001:** the internal `mechanism='rms'` duration/distance/trip payouts (settle to OUR OWN connect account) → **2130 Connect-Account Clearing** (internal transfer, NOT a cost; nets via the $1000-rule cash-back, FLOW-26). Consistent with POL-52/DQ-32 (misc = description-only). Corrects earlier "5001 = mechanism='rms' duration/distance" — that was the internal transfer, not the owner payable. *(Gaurav, 2026-07-28)*
- **POL-57** STRIPE CONNECT ACCOUNTS ARE BANKS — no 2130 clearing (Gaurav, 2026-07-28). The Stripe Connect accounts ALREADY exist as bank accounts: **1018 (SG, entity 2) / 1020 (AU, entity 3)** (+ Stripe Platform 1017/1019). So **DROP the "2130 Connect-Account Clearing"** — internal RMS earnings flow through the connect BANK accounts (1018/1020), NOT a clearing account. **SYNC = view-fed:** inflow ← the cash transfers landing in our connect accounts (from the transfer views); outflow ← the sweep to the operating bank (OCBC-3001/Fleet), recognized via the $1000-rule (FLOW-26) as INTERNAL_TRANSFER on the receiving side. **COMPLETENESS GUARANTEE = balance reconciliation:** opening + inflows − outflows = closing must equal Stripe's actual reported connect-account balance on that date (same discipline as OCBC/CBA) — that's how to-and-fro capture is guaranteed. Result: internal RMS = bank activity (NO P&L); **5001 stays clean = real owner payments only** (`rms` type + owner bank rules Bombora/CDC/Norman/Abwin). Verification/deposit connect accounts = SEPARATE stream (later). **EXECUTED 2026-07-28:** 5000-5003 renamed "Host Trip Earnings"; 2130 NOT created; internal RMS → connect bank 1018/1020 via the BANK path (Stripe balance-transaction feed + INTERNAL_TRANSFER rules, NOT a cost JE — avoids double-count); `view_{mkt}_c_connect_balance_recon` built + ties to cent (SG net-flow −S$58,977 / AU −A$27,578 H1); 5001 = owner-only (SG S$7,286 + AU owner-rules A$221k); template 29 retired, 72/73 (Dr 5001/Cr 2120) active, 74/75 deleted; rules 377 Bombora/378 CDC/379 Norman/40 Abwin → 5001. Nothing posted live. **Connect inflow wired as economic-event `connect_internal_transfer`** (Gaurav: NOT "deposit" — avoids confusion with customer deposits/verification): Dr 1018/1020 Connect / Cr 1017/1019 Platform = INTERNAL TRANSFER (no P&L), posts on Stripe-sync projection; outflow (Connect→OCBC-3001/Fleet) via the $1000-rule (rules 30/214). Where Stripe cash movements sit = the economic-event lane (`_c_` views → view_map → je_templates → JEs, against Platform bank 1017/1019); the connect flow joins the same lane. *(Gaurav, 2026-07-28)*
- **POL-58** SG NEGATIVE FUEL → HOST FUEL PAYABLE CONTRA (Gaurav, 2026-07-29). SG negative `fuel_charge` (collected from host, ~−S$2-4k H1, ticket-linked "excess fuel pay") settles against the **host fuel PAYABLE (2120) as a negative** — REDUCES what we owe the host for fuel, **NOT revenue** (contrast AU POL-51 where a negative fuel = 4032 revenue). So SG fuel host cost nets: `fuel_inclusion` (5023, Cr 2120) − `fuel_charge` collected (Dr 2120). Restores FLOW-11 stream-3 netting for SG; **AU is the exception (POL-51, 4032 revenue).** Also: **5037 Cost of Fuel Refund to Guest source = `view_AU_c_fuel_refunds`** (Stripe card refunds, A$21,791 H1), NOT the fuel_refund payoutType (Gaurav, 2026-07-29). *(Gaurav, 2026-07-29)*
- **POL-59** FINAL FUEL ROUTING both markets + 4029 RETIRED (Gaurav, 2026-07-29). **SG (all host-fuel nets in 5023):** guest fuel charge→**4023** (rev); `fuel_inclusion` (pay host)→**5023**; `fuel_charge` collected-from-host→**REDUCES 5023** (contra to fuel cost — NOT 2120; **corrects POL-58**); `fuel_refund` (pay host)→**5023**. So SG 5023 = inclusion − charge-collected + refund. **AU:** guest fuel charge→**4023**; NO fuel_inclusion; negative `fuel_charge`/`fuel_refund` (charge host)→**4032** (rev, POL-51); positive `fuel_refund` (pay host)→**5023** (cost); guest fuel refund (Stripe card, `view_AU_c_fuel_refunds`)→**5037** (cost). SG/AU asymmetry: host-fuel-charge books SG→5023-reduction vs AU→4032-revenue. **4029 "Incidentals Revenue – Fuel Refund" (POL-43) → RETIRE** — redundant; created on the old assumption guest-fuel-refund=revenue-contra, but now guest fuel refund→5037 (cost), nothing feeds 4029. FUEL FULLY CLOSED both markets. *(Gaurav, 2026-07-29)*
- **POL-60** MISC_PAYOUT FINAL ROUTING both markets (Gaurav, 2026-07-29). Classify `misc_payout` by DESCRIPTION (POL-52), market-specific (DQ-32). **Carve-outs (both markets):** tolls→5020 · towing→5033 · damage/key/storage/repair→5021 · sticker→5041 · superhost→5040 · downtime→5043 · parking→5060. **AU-specific:** trip/distance CORRECTIONS ("correct/missing distance","rebilling","gap hours","additional days","additional earnings")→**5000/5001 Host Trip Earnings** (mechanism split; RMS-mechanism follows connect-clearing POL-57) · AFCA→**5021** · goodwill/compensation→**5042**. **SG-specific:** loss-of-use→**5021** · "correction for long term discount"→**5042** · goodwill→**5042**. **Everything else → 5042 Misc.** NO new accounts needed (all map to existing 5xxx). Completion = build the market-specific misc classifier views + wire to templates. *(Gaurav, 2026-07-29)*
- **FLOW-27** TWO KINDS OF DISCOUNTS (Gaurav, 2026-07-29). Drive lah gives TWO distinct discount types: **(1) COMPANY discounts** — promos/marketing Drive lah gives to guests; **the revenue currently shown is already NET of these** → to present properly we must GROSS UP revenue and show the company discount as its own line (SEPARATE revenue-side task, not yet calculated — Midas's arc). **(2) HOST discounts** — discounts the car OWNERS/hosts give on their own **REGULAR rentals** (`long_term_discount` = a longer-booking discount hosts give; **NOT Flex+**; SG −S$42,357 H1). We **RECOVER these from the host** (deduct from what we owe them) → a **separate line under COST OF SALES: 5050 renamed "Host Discounts"** that **reduces host payables (2120)**: Dr 2120 / Cr 5050, the host bears their own discount. **`promo_discount` payoutType (SG S$904,742 all-time, ZERO H1) is a LEGACY mislabeled catch-all** (descriptions = "Payouts done for Disputes/Shortfalls etc") — NOT a real promo discount; Phase-B only, ignore for H1. *(Gaurav, 2026-07-29)*
- **DQ-33** ACCOUNT_VIEW_MAP review corrections (Gaurav marked-up xlsx, 2026-07-29). **(1) VERIFICATION CHARGE is NOT revenue** — `view_{mkt}_c_verification_charge_received` must NOT feed 4025; it's cash-in that gets refunded = a **LIABILITY we return** (book like a deposit, 2110-family, netted by verification refunds). FIX: remove from 4025. **(2) CONNECT-CLEARING views are CASH not accrual** — rename `view_{mkt}_a_host_rms_internal_clearing` → `_c_` (they're cash movements, POL-57). **(3) RMS REVENUE split (4001) = by CONNECT ACCOUNTS (ENT-7), NOT host earnings** — the ACCOUNT_VIEW_MAP note "RMS margin via host earnings" was WRONG; revenue P2P/RMS split is connect-account-owned (same as the trip revenue split). **(4) AU FUEL CHARGE (4023) comes from INCIDENTAL INVOICES**, not "inside trip cash" — needs an AU incidentals-invoice fuel source. **(5) 4002 Flex+ revenue = subscription charges + Flex+ incidentals invoices; 4003 Flex+ RMS = split by CAR-PLATE numbers in the description.** **(6) 4011 insurance subscription must be built/separated from total subscription.** **(7) 5055 should NOT all fold into 5054 — discuss.** **(8) MISC IS DESCRIPTION-ONLY, NEVER host-type/RMS split (Gaurav, firm — reaffirms POL-56):** Atlas's AU `misc_corrections_rms_clearing` (mechanism-map split of corrections → 1020 clearing) is WRONG → REMOVE it; the trip/distance CORRECTIONS route **ALL to 5000** (single Host Trip Earnings line, by description, no P2P/RMS split). Misc never touches connect/mechanism logic. **(9) 4025** = incidentals-invoice "Other" residual (Stripe) + direct guest payments (POL-41; PayNow = bank-rule, not a view) — verification stripped out (correction 1). *(Gaurav, 2026-07-29)*
- **DQ-34** REVENUE ARC WIRED (Midas, 2026-07-29; nothing posted live). - **FLOW-28** PAYMENT PLANS — how they work today (Gaurav confirmed, 2026-07-29; NO action needed, record-only). A payment plan = a customer pays an invoice in **instalments**. Treatment in the current system: **(1) Revenue accrual EXCLUDES payment_plan** — `subscription_other`/accrual views filter out `metadata.payment_plan != ''` OR lines LIKE '%payment plan%' (FLOW-21) so revenue is NOT recognized twice. **(2) The CASH-paid views DO capture payment-plan payments** — `view_{MKT}_c_subscription_invoice_paid` (filter: charge, invoice NOT NULL, subscription NOT NULL; does NOT exclude payment_plan) books the cash Dr 1017/1019 / Cr 1200 = receivable settlement. So the net today: revenue recognized once on the underlying invoice; payment-plan cash flows through the invoice-paid lane as an AR settlement, not new revenue. H1 AU payment_plan ≈ 45,627.81 (accrual-view figure) / 61,830.97 (all payment_plan invoices incl. non-subscription). `view_{MKT}_a_subscription_payment_plan` views exist to ISOLATE these lines (parked, not retired — the receivable-settlement hook). *(Gaurav, 2026-07-29)*
- **POL-64** VERIFICATION + DEPOSIT = ONE held-funds account per market, BALANCE-SHEET only (Gaurav, 2026-07-29). Verification & deposits are money we HOLD and OWE BACK — **NOT P&L** (never revenue/expense). Both currently exist in AU; SG has verification now, deposit historically (2019–2021). Design: **ONE "Stripe Connect – Customer Held Funds" COA bank account PER MARKET** (deposit + verification combined into one, NOT separate). Double-entry: **receipt** Dr [Held-Funds Connect a/c, asset] / Cr **2110** Customer Deposits Held; **refund** Dr 2110 / Cr [Held-Funds]; **sweep to operating bank** (CBA/OCBC) Dr bank / Cr [Held-Funds] = internal transfer (2110 untouched — liability clears only on customer refund). 2110 recognized AT RECEIPT (not at sweep, fixing FLOW-13). Only P&L touch = Stripe fee on charge → 5010. **Implementation:** repoint the 4 templates (deposits_received, deposit_refunds, verification_charge_received, verification_refunds) off Platform 1017/1019 onto the new held-funds account. Fixes FLOW-24 GAP-2 (inflows now captured) + makes held balance visible + reconcilable via the `connect_balance_recon` pattern. **EXECUTED 2026-07-29 (Gaurav approved):** created COA **1021** "Bank - Stripe (Customer Held Funds)" SG/entity2 (bank acct 1657, num acct_1EhuMG SGD) + **1022** AU/entity3 (bank acct 1658, num acct_1JkKmQ AUD), both ASSET/DEBIT/Cash-Bank mirroring 1018/1020. Repointed 6 template legs off Platform: deposits_received Dr 1021(SG)/1022(AU), deposit_refunds Cr 1021/1022, verification_charge_received Dr 1022(AU), verification_refunds Cr 1022(AU) — all vs 2110. Nothing posted live (templates affect future staging/projection; posted H1 baseline still on Platform 1017/1019 → re-projects to held-funds at RA-10 cutover). *(Gaurav, 2026-07-29)*

SG deposits STOPPED ~2021 (historical). ~~NB deposit account sits under the **Ventures** holding entity, not SG operating.~~ (struck — see ENT-22) AU deposit = `acct_1JkKmQ` (singhal.gaurav@gmail.com, 2021-10-14, "Drive mate", FLOW-13). **SG HAS NO VERIFICATION — verified: `sg_stripe_balance_transactions` has 0 rows matching 'verif' (AU has 93,350, net A$26). ENT-7's "SG verification = it@drivelah.sg" was an UNFOUNDED ASSUMPTION — struck.** `it@drivelah.sg` (`acct_1Skjf3K`, created 2026-01-01, "DRIVE LAH PTE. LTD.") purpose UNKNOWN (no verification data attributed; a generic 2026 SG custom account) — do NOT label it. So the SG held-funds account (POL-64) covers **deposit only** (historical, drive.lah2018); AU covers deposit + verification. **AU verification confirmed (self-checked): settles to `acct_1JkKmQQWb9mOwfae` — the SAME account as AU deposit** (verification charges carry `on_behalf_of` + `transfer_data.destination` = acct_1JkKmQ; 57,096 charges). So the physical held-funds Stripe account is ONE per market: **SG ← `acct_1EhuMG` (drive.lah2018, deposit only)**; **AU ← `acct_1JkKmQ` (deposit + verification)**. AU verification is LIVE/ongoing (~1,500–4,000 charges/mo through Jul-2026); gross nets ~$0 ($1 refundable auth) but consumes ~A$14,947 net Stripe FEES over the period → the only P&L touch = fees to 5010 (POL-64). Verification sits in `au_stripe_balance_transactions` (platform table), 0 rows in the connected-accounts table (on_behalf_of destination-charge quirk) — economic owner is still acct_1JkKmQ. Balance-transaction tables (`{mkt}_stripe_balance_transactions_for_connected_accounts`) do NOT tag which connected account a row belongs to → account identity comes from `{mkt}_stripe_connected_accounts` registry (id/email/created/business_profile). *(Gaurav Q + verified, 2026-07-29)*
- **POL-65** VIEW NAMING CONVENTION — canonical `v_` prefix (Gaurav, 2026-07-29). All views USED in the new economic-events system get renamed to a **`v_` prefix** (lowercase v + underscore) so new/canonical (`v_…`) is instantly distinguishable from old/legacy (`view_…`). Core transform (safe, no collisions): `view_`→`v_`, drop trailing `_new` (kills the SG/AU asymmetry). Canonical scheme: `v_{MKT}_{lane}_{domain}_{category}[_{qualifier}]` — lane `a`=accrual/P&L · `c`=cash/settlement · `x`=internal/clearing (no P&L); drop redundant `_invoiced`/`_paid` (lane already encodes it) as OPTIONAL deeper normalization. Legacy `view_…` names stay in place (not deleted, per Gaurav — marked legacy/retire). Rename = create `v_` view + repoint view_map.py/PAYOUT_LINE_VIEWS/feeder SQL; purely cosmetic, no Dr/Cr change, nothing posts. Map: `documentation/wip/VIEW_RENAME_MAP.xlsx` (125 used views, 0 collisions). **EXECUTED 2026-07-29 (Ledger, verified):** 125 used views renamed `view_`→`v_` (core transform, `_new` dropped), created in dependency order, GATE tie-outs matched to the cent (30 views), view_map.py repointed (119 v_ refs; 0 missing in ClickHouse), 122 old used names DROPPED. **3 shared feeders FULLY RENAMED** (`v_host_mechanism_map`, `v_{SG,AU}_a_misc_payout_classified`): the 8 legacy referrers (6 parked/dead host_payout_flexplus/regular_rms + the 2 bare `view_{SG,AU}_a_misc_`) were repointed to the v_ feeders (row counts unchanged), then the 3 old feeder names DROPPED (Gaurav: v_ must mean in-use, no shared feeder on a view_ name). Final: **125 v_ (all in-use incl. feeders) · 44 view_ (legacy/unused only) · 0 old feeder names**. **2 view_map refs still `view_`** (`view_{SG,AU}_a_incidentals_direct_revenue[_new]`) — the abandoned trap views (remark C, no active template, outside used-set). 47 legacy `view_` remain (44 unused + 3 shared feeders). Nothing posted. NEXT (step 2, Gaurav): consolidate view docs into KNOWLEDGE.md + purge stale view knowledge, referencing the final v_ names. *(Gaurav, 2026-07-29)*
- **DQ-46** DISTANCE-BY-RATIO P2P/RMS SPLIT LOST in the source-split re-architecture (Gaurav Q + verified, 2026-07-29). OLD posted system (FLOW-19): AU distance revenue split by the month's BASE P2P/RMS ratio via monthly reclass — AU H1 RMS 211,342 INCLUDED ~34,543 distance-by-ratio → 4001. NEW source-split views: `view_AU_a_trip_revenue_rms` EXCLUDES distance (`processName NOT IN ('distance-charging','unpaid-dues')`); distance flows via its own event_types `trip_distance_invoiced` (Dr 1200/Cr **4000**) + `trip_distance_cash_collected` (Dr 1019/Cr **4000**) — ALL to 4000 P2P, NONE to 4001 RMS. CONSEQUENCE: new system under-states 4001 RMS + over-states 4000 P2P by the distance-RMS portion (~A$34.5k H1). SG has no distance. **DECISION NEEDED (separate task, not naming):** reinstate the distance-by-ratio reclass (distance→4001 by month's base ratio, FLOW-19) OR accept distance all-in-4000. *(Gaurav, 2026-07-29)*
- **POL-66** SYNC BEHAVIOR CONTRACT (Gaurav, 2026-07-29, confirmed). Trigger = the **Stripe Platform bank-account "sync" button** (per entity; SG 1017 / AU 1019) — same gesture as Wise accounts, but for Stripe it runs the economic-events lane. **Behavior:** (1) `stage_month` across months (first run all history; later incremental + overlap) → economic events STAGED; (2) `import_payout_lines` → Stripe payout cash as bank txns on the Platform account; **nothing posts**. `project` (post) is a SEPARATE deliberate step. **Two idempotency contracts:** **Economic events** — every sync RECOMPUTES from the views; unposted rows OVERWRITTEN (latest view wins); **POSTED rows immutable** — same amount = left alone, different amount = flagged **MISMATCH** (payload records new amount, ledger untouched), never silent re-post; JEs change ONLY via `project`, and `project` only acts on STAGED rows. **Payout lines** — NEVER recompute (each is a fixed Stripe fact); dedup by `balance_transaction_id`; already-imported (categorized/posted OR not) = SKIPPED; only new lines appended; no MISMATCH concept. Per-month `/stage` stays as a surgical tool. Cutover = wipe + re-sync + project (clean slate). *(Gaurav, 2026-07-29)*
- **FLOW-29** THE STRIPE→BANK $1000-RULE PAIR — exact accounts (verified, 2026-07-29). Both rules match bank description `CONTAINS 'CSDB STRIPE PAYMENTS'`, split by amount: **Rule 30 "3001 Incoming from Stripe Transfer" (≥ $999.99)** → **Dr bank(1016) / Cr 1017 Stripe PLATFORM** = large platform sweeps (H1: 30 JEs, S$482,000). **Rule 214 "Fleet earnings" (< $1000)** ← THE SUB-$1000 RULE → **Dr bank(1000) / Cr 1018 Stripe CONNECT (SGD)** = small fleet/RMS earnings settling out of the connect account (H1: 3 JEs, S$1,861.63). So: big = platform sweep (Cr 1017); small = connect fleet-earnings (Cr 1018/1020). **Rule-214 JEs are STANDALONE** (fire on the bank txn alone, NOT paired with a Stripe payout) → they credit the connect account with NO connect-side transaction (confirms Gaurav's design), and deleting Stripe payouts does NOT touch them. **STRIPE CLEAN-SLATE DELETION (Gaurav, 2026-07-30, month-by-month):** per month delete economic events + their `economic_events` JEs + Stripe payout txns + their `categorization_engine` transfer JEs, and revert the paired `file_import` bank legs → IMPORTED. KEEP: platform opening JEs (1017/1019, verified correct) + Rule-214 connect JEs. Then re-sync month-by-month + reconcile 3 Stripe balances (platform / held-funds 1021-1022 / connect 1018-1020) vs Stripe API. *(Gaurav, 2026-07-30)*
- **FLOW-30** THE RMS CASH MODEL — three distinct lanes, currently conflated (Gaurav, 2026-07-30). Drive lah operates its own fleet ("RMS") through **its own Stripe connected accounts** — identifiable in `{mkt}_stripe_connected_accounts` by email pattern `%flexplus%` OR `%caretaker%` OR `match(email,'rms[0-9]')` (e.g. `rms3.drivelah@gmail.com`); `v_host_mechanism_map` already uses exactly this test to tag a host `rms` vs `p2p`. SG has 95 such accounts, AU 12 (⚠ regex may false-positive a real host like `ansarms1@yahoo.com` — eyeball the list). **The three lanes that MUST be separated:** ① **Internal transfers to our own connect accounts** — platform → our RMS pool. AUTHORITATIVE SOURCE = the actual **Stripe transfers** (`{mkt}_stripe_transfers` where `destination IN our-own-accts`), CASH basis (created date), NOT the payout_entries trip-end accrual. Books **Dr 1018 / Cr 1017**. SG Jan = −$9,668.53 (91 transfers), AU Jan = −$4,376.17 (50). MUST be **excluded from both** the real-host cash-transfers view **and** Host Payables (2120). ② **Real P2P host payouts** — transfers to genuine (non-ours) connected accounts; this is what `host_transfers_cash` should be after the exclusion; settles 2120. SG Jan real-host = $594,709.28, AU = $566,972.87. ③ **Real RMS host cost (5001)** — what drive lah pays the actual car OWNERS — comes from EXACTLY TWO places: (a) `payout_entries` where `payoutType = RMS` OR leaked-from-miscellaneous-payout, and (b) **direct bank transfers** to owners (off-Stripe). Nothing else. *(Gaurav, 2026-07-30)*
- **DQ-47** `host_transfers_cash` (`v_{mkt}_c_host_transfers`) nets EVERY `transfer`/`transfer_reversal`/`platform_earning`/`platform_earning_refund` balance-txn with **zero destination filter**, so it silently includes the platform→our-own-connect-account internal transfers (FLOW-30 ①). Compounding it, `connect_internal_transfer` (sourced from `v_{mkt}_c_host_rms_internal_clearing`, payout_entries mechanism=rms, TRIP-END accrual basis) books a SECOND Dr 1018 / **Cr 1017** for the same money → the platform account 1017/1019 is credited twice for RMS. This double-credit IS the Jan platform-reconciliation residual (CONNECT bucket −6,277.39 SG / −7,363.50 AU; total gap −6,495.50 / −12,121.79). The balance-txns table has no destination column — the destination lives only in `{mkt}_stripe_transfers`. *(self, 2026-07-30)*
- **POL-67** RMS RE-ARCHITECTURE — the fix for DQ-47/FLOW-30 (approved approach, Gaurav 2026-07-30): (A) rebuild `host_transfers_cash` to EXCLUDE our-own-account destinations → pure real-host payouts (Dr 2120 / Cr 1017); (B) re-source `connect_internal_transfer` from `{mkt}_stripe_transfers` filtered to our-own-accts, CASH basis → Dr 1018 / Cr 1017 booked ONCE at the real cash amount; (C) stop `host_trip_earnings_rms` crediting 2120 — RMS never rides the P2P payable lane; the real 5001 cost is FLOW-30 ③ (payout_entries RMS/misc-leak + direct bank transfers). Net effect: 1017 credited once cash-basis → platform reconciles; 2120 becomes pure P2P and self-nets; 1018 pool builds correctly. Interlocks with task #17 (5001 rework). *(Gaurav, 2026-07-30)*
- **POL-68** THREE-STRIPE-BALANCE RECON MODEL — Gaurav's SIMPLIFIED, FINAL ruling (2026-07-30), supersedes the deposit-repoint half of DQ-49 and the deposit-lane half of DQ-48: **① PLATFORM (1017/1019)** = Σ platform balance-txns that land on it — reconciled (POL-67 A+B: host_transfers excludes RMS+deposit dests; connect_internal cash-basis; RMS+reserve credit legs stay on 1017). **② DEPOSIT/held-funds (1021/1022)** = **inflow `deposits_received` + `verification_charge_received`, outflow `deposit_refunds` + `verification_refunds` — those four views ARE the whole reconciliation.** KEEP their current templates on 1021/1022 (Dr 1022/Cr 2110 etc.); do NOT repoint to platform; do NOT build a deposit-transfer lane (the platform→deposit-account transfer is just the same money's plumbing — the charge/refund views already capture it). The `deposit_internal_transfer` lane built earlier this session was REMOVED. **③ CONNECT POOL (1018/1020)** = cash-in `connect_internal_transfer` (RMS transfers, built) − cash-out **already handled by the sub-$1000 Rule 214** when the pool settles to OCBC (FLOW-29) — NO separate Stripe payout-out leg to build ("you don't need to hold those transactions"). No overlap: each transfer routes by destination to exactly one account. Jan platform residual after this model: SG +442.05 / AU −6,820.84 = the 4 unbooked edge cats + deposit-view filter precision. *(Gaurav, 2026-07-30)*

After POL-67/68 + these fixes, EVERY lane ties to the cent — CHG, REF, HTX, CONN, RSV, DIS, PAY, ADJ — except a diffuse charge-timing residual: **SG −18.40 (0.003%), AU −665.72 (0.15%)** (AU = ~$569 spread across 2,000+ trip charges at TZ/filter boundaries + ~$96 fee; not a missing category). (4) all AU c-views TZ-aligned to Sydney (host_transfers, rms_internal, disputes) so month boundaries match. **POSTED 2026-07-30** — Jan re-staged+re-projected (deleted 8 stale JEs, re-posted from new views); verified on PURE POSTED JEs identical to proof (SG −18.40 / AU −665.72); integrity 0 unbalanced JEs / 102 events POSTED / 0 orphans. Posting-sign note: disputes+adjustments views report `-sum(net)` with `flip_on_negative=true` (projector only flips negatives; this makes posted 1017/1019 impact = the Stripe net for BOTH signs). NOTE: deposit-transfer re-booking to 1021/1022 must not double-count against the deposit CHARGE events already pointed at 1021/1022 — resolve as part of the held-funds (1021/1022) reconciliation step, not the platform step. *(Gaurav, 2026-07-30)*
- **FLOW-31** THE THREE STRIPE BALANCES — how each reconciles (verified 2026-07-30). ① **PLATFORM (1017/1019):** Σ platform balance-txns (`{mkt}_stripe_balance_transactions`), cash basis — DONE (POL-67 A+B). ② **DEPOSIT/held-funds (1021/1022):** the deposit connected account (SG `acct_1EhuMGAcVqeggTlg` dead, AU `acct_1JkKmQQWb9mOwfae` active) has **ZERO payouts** — it only holds & releases back to platform. So its authoritative balance = **net deposit transfers ONLY** (`v_{mkt}_c_deposit_internal_transfer`). ③ **CONNECT POOL (1018/1020):** the RMS operator accounts have **BOTH** legs — cash IN = RMS transfers from platform (`v_{mkt}_c_host_rms_internal_clearing`), cash OUT = **payouts to owner banks** (`{mkt}_stripe_payouts` where `connected_account_id IN` our-rms-accts; SG 1,311.53 Jan / 23,634 all-time; AU 269 all-time). To reconcile 1018 BOTH legs must post. NO OVERLAP between ② and ③ — a transfer is routed by destination to exactly one account. *(Gaurav, 2026-07-30)*
- **DQ-49** DEPOSIT DOUBLE-COUNT — the deposit/verification CHARGE lands on the PLATFORM, not the deposit account (verified 2026-07-30). Both `v_AU_c_customer_deposits_received` (desc `%Deposit%`) and `v_AU_c_verification_charge_received` (desc `%Verifications Charge%`) read `au_stripe_balance_transactions` `reporting_category='charge'` — i.e. platform charges. The money is THEN transferred to the deposit account (the ~$20 holds → `acct_1JkKmQ`). But the current templates book `deposits_received`/`verification_charge_received` as **Dr 1022 / Cr 2110**, skipping the platform hop. Adding `deposit_internal_transfer` (Dr 1022 / Cr 1019) would DOUBLE the 1022 inflow. CORRECT MODEL (amends POL-64): charge → **Dr 1019 platform / Cr 2110 liability**; transfer → **Dr 1022 held / Cr 1019 platform**; release/refund reverses both. Then platform nets (charge in − transfer out) and 1022 = transfers only → both reconcile, no overlap. Needs Gaurav approval (POL-64 change). *(self, 2026-07-30)*
- **FLOW-12** AU insurance CLAIMS CYCLE (Gaurav, 2026-07-27): on an accident ① the GUEST is charged the excess (incidentals invoicing lane, 1200 → 4025-family — the main feeder of AU receivables) ② the INSURER pays the claim — GT Insurance's bank wording is "Direct Credit 650646 **GLOBAL TRANSPORT**" (same company as cp 76 GT insurance; artifact cp 330 retired) and its claims administrator pays as "Direct Credit 509196 **INSURET CLAIM** <ref>" ③ the HOST is paid for damage gross via host_damage_payout events (5021). Booking law: claim receipts → **4030 Insurance Recoveries (income line — Gaurav ruling, kept as income "for now"; NOT netted against premiums, NOT P2P revenue)**; insurer COMMISSION receipts (SG Income Insurance) stay 7000. Deterministic: incoming rule #374 → 4030; outgoing premiums/excesses per POL-32 rules #372/#373. *(Gaurav, 2026-07-27)*

- **FLOW-11** Fuel has THREE distinct economic streams: (1) guests pay fuel via Stripe (view_SG_c_trip_fuel_cash_collected, ~S$63k/H1-26) = fuel REVENUE (4000); (2) the fuel-inclusion product pays hosts (payoutType=fuel_inclusion, ~S$79k/H1-26) = fuel COGS (5023); (3) fuel charges collected FROM hosts by payout deduction (~S$2k/H1-26) net off host payable (2120→4000). The old sync conflated (1) and (3). *(view lineage audit, 2026-07-25)*

## 5. Data quirks & traps (DQ)

- **CP-33** REVIO LABS PTE. LTD. = Gaurav's other company (related party, NOT a Drive lah entity — never enters IC accounts) and it banks with WISE, so an OCBC FAST payment to Revio prints "FAST PAYMENT OTHR WISE …" — indistinguishable from our own Wise top-ups by wording. Only 2026 flow: 2026-06-16 mistaken 10,000 FAST payment from OCBC 3001 to Revio's Wise account, refunded by Revio 2026-06-18 ("…2193987552Refund"); booked as a direct NET-OFF — one JE (5936) Dr/Cr the bank's own COA with both statement lines attached; mistaken-payment-and-refund pairs never touch receivables or P&L (Gaurav ruling). Trap: "OTHR WISE" wording proves the beneficiary BANK is Wise, not that the money went to OUR Wise. *(Gaurav + statement evidence, 2026-07-27)*
- **DQ-64** Counterparty cleanup backlog (631 total: 478 vendor / 119 employee / 16 investor / 10 government / 5 customer / 2 other / 1 bank). (a) 319 of 631 (~50%) have NO aliases → categorization L1 exact/alias matching under-fires; alias enrichment is the recurring safeguard (task #6). (b) 7 near-duplicate name groups: case dupes (Autorola / AUTOROLA); same person as BOTH employee AND vendor (Anas Ashfaq, Jay Balan, Abdul Saaqib, Jay); IRAS as both government AND vendor; Riezel Basilio Villanueva duplicated employee/employee. Merges need approval (POL-46, key changes). *(DB-verified, 2026-07-31)*
- **DQ-65** On-ground team payee identities (Gaurav, 2026-07-31): "Jennilyn Reyes" (cp 555, remittance name) = HR employee **Jen Reyes (cp 197, jen@drivemate.au)** — merge 555→197. "Gem C. Rey-Hipolito" (cp 538) is a DISTINCT person — NOT the same as Gem Antonio (259); keep 538 as-is, do not merge. *(Gaurav, 2026-07-31)*
- **DQ-66** Historical-reconciliation seeding cross-attributed near-identical payment sets to different on-ground payees: cp 538 (Gem C. Rey-Hipolito) and cp 196 (Jennilyn Palacol Labajos) share ~11 of 13 transactions identical to the cent (same date+amount). Person-level payee attribution for on-ground contractor payments (COA 5063) is therefore unreliable in the seed; trust HR-synced records (with external_id/email) over payment-only counterparties. *(DB-verified, 2026-07-31)*
- **DQ-70** Retool `finance_db` attachments use TWO storage mechanisms: (a) **direct public S3 URL** `https://retool-storage-aman.s3…/public/finance-tool/…` (newer rows, ~Sept-2025+; fetchable directly or via the S3 resource `getSignedUrl` on the `public/finance-tool/<key>`), and (b) **Retool built-in File Storage UUID** (older rows; opaque — not a key in any org S3 bucket, only resolvable via `retoolStorage.getSignedUrl(id)` inside a Retool app/workflow — the MCP sandbox has no `retoolStorage` global). The original 1,342-invoice ingest consumed a manifest whose UUIDs were already resolved to signed URLs inside Retool (1,006 uuid + 336 url). *(verified, 2026-08-01)*
- **DQ-71** Retool 3rd-party-payment backlog reconciliation (2026-08-01): 1,759 total; 1,342 already have the file. The 417 no-file backlog = **9 live-URL** (fetchable now — but 7 turned out to be dupes we already hold / 2 are 404) + **94 UUID** (need the Retool resolver) + **314 no-document** (198 no attachment + 116 broken `.../null` URL — nothing to fetch). So only ~94 recoverable docs remain, behind the resolver. *(verified, 2026-08-01)*
- **DQ-73** admin-bff permission modules — `users` ≠ `user-mgmt` (Gaurav, 2026-08-01). They are TWO distinct modules, both kept: **`users`** = MARKETPLACE users (Drive lah hosts/guests, a product-admin area; 47 live grants, currently ungated like the other product modules); **`user-mgmt`** = CONSOLE operators (our internal team who log into the admin console + their permissions/roles; gated by routes). Do NOT remap `users`→`user-mgmt` — an earlier read mistook them for a duplicate; they are separate axes (customer-facing vs staff-facing). *(Gaurav, 2026-08-01)*
- **DQ-72** finance-api Postgres is NOT local — it's a **deployed AWS RDS** instance and this is **intentionally SHARED with collections** (Gaurav, 2026-08-01). `.env` `DATABASE_URL` = `postgresql://collectionsagent@collections-db.compunokr5xr.ap-southeast-2.rds.amazonaws.com:5432/collections-db?gssencmode=disable` (Sydney region). Resolution: `src/database.py:20 get_database_url()` returns `DATABASE_URL` verbatim if set (the `DB_HOST`/localhost component fallback only fires when it's unset). So ALL finance data (invoices, 1,759 Retool rows, migrations through 051) lives on this shared RDS, not on the laptop. IMPLICATION for "push finance to remote so the team can access it": the DB is already remote+shared, so the remaining work is (1) DEPLOY the finance-api Flask service (currently only local :8081) with the same `DATABASE_URL`, (2) point admin-bff `FINANCE_API_URL` at it (default is `http://localhost:8081`, `config.ts:20`), (3) grant team `finance:write` in `user_permissions` (or `GOOGLE_ADMIN_EMAIL` super-admin). NB no finance staging/prod separation — team edits land on the same rows as H1 finalization, by design. *(Gaurav, 2026-08-01)*
- **POL-82** STANDARD FLAG `recon.document_status = "NO_DOCUMENT"` marks an invoice whose Retool source row has NO attachable file (no attachment or broken URL). This is DISTINCT from `recon.document_gate = "not_invoice"` (a document exists but is not an invoice). One-time backfill: applied to 218 existing stubs 2026-08-01. Future uploads always carry a doc, so this is a historical marker only. *(Gaurav ruling, 2026-08-01)*
- **POL-83** DUPLICATES ALLOWED AT DRAFT (Gaurav ruling, 2026-08-01): every distinct Retool row is imported as its own draft invoice — EVEN when it's a duplicate invoice/payment — and FLAGGED (`recon.duplicate.{is_duplicate,duplicate_of,dup_reason}`); never hard-blocked. Only PROMOTION past draft is blocked (submit() gate + active-only unique indexes). Fix: migration 051 made `ix_finance_invoices_pdf_content_hash` ACTIVE-ONLY (was plain unique → wrongly hard-blocked duplicate-file drafts, mirroring the semantic index from mig 046). Both file-hash AND semantic duplicates may now coexist as draft/void/rejected; two live invoices still can't share a file or a semantic key. Applies identically to the 94 UUID backlog when resolved. *(built + verified: 5 AFCA/Focussed re-imported as flagged-dup drafts #2351-2355, 2026-08-01)*
- **POL-84** ATTACH-TO-ROW + duplicate-uploads-allowed (Gaurav ruling, 2026-08-01): (1) A team member fills a no-document stub IN PLACE via `POST /invoices/<id>/attach` (multipart) — `invoice_service.attach_document` hashes→extracts→vendor-matches→uploads→backfills the SAME row→clears document_status/stub→runs dedup→flags if duplicate. Never creates a new row (fresh upload is only for genuinely new invoices). FE: "Attach document" control in the edit modal's preview pane when the row has no file. (2) The interactive upload path now MATCHES the ingest principle — duplicates are ALLOWED at draft, FLAGGED, and blocked only at promotion: `/extract` no longer 409s (advisory `duplicate_check` + `exact_file_match` instead); `invoice_service.create` no longer rejects a semantic duplicate — it creates + flags `recon.duplicate`; `submit()` still hard-blocks promoting a hard-duplicate (first-one-wins). Backed by the active-only hash+semantic indexes (mig 046/051). *(built + tested: 25 invoice/dup tests green; FE tsc clean, not pixel-viewed, 2026-08-01)*

Consistent across all 23 statements 2022–2025 (12,006 rows, 100% balance-chain verified). The statements also chain ACROSS quarters (each opening = prior closing) and each carries its own reconciliation equation line (opening − debits + credits = closing). *(verified, 2026-07-26)*

- **POL-69** FEE PRINCIPLE — book everything GROSS, count fees ONCE, never book any category at net. Every economic-event view that touches the Stripe Platform books the **gross** `amount` (not `net`). All Stripe fees are a single line, `stripe_fees_paid` = `Σ(fee column across every balance-txn row except connect_reserved_funds)` + `Σ(abs(amount) of standalone reporting_category='fee' rows)`. Because `net = amount − fee` row-by-row, `Σ gross − stripe_fees = Σ net` closes the platform automatically with zero double-count. The trap that motivated this: categories booked at net (disputes were `−sum(net)`, so the dispute fee was already embedded) while stripe_fees ALSO summed that fee → double-count. Fix (2026-07-31): converted `v_{AU,SG}_c_disputes`, `_stripe_reserve`, `_stripe_platform_adjustments` from `net`→`amount` (reserve/adjustments are fee=0 no-ops; disputes carried real fee — AU +3,777.50, SG +285.00 H1) and reverted the fee views to the clean sum. Applied identically to AU and SG. *(Gaurav, 2026-07-31)*
- **DQ-53** PLATFORM RECON METRIC — the economic-events lane residual must compare our platform-account (1019 AU / 1017 SG) impact against authoritative Stripe net **EXCLUDING `reporting_category IN ('connect_reserved_funds','payout')`**. `connect_reserved_funds` is the reserve (separate account 1023/1024); `payout` is the cash sweep to our external bank, carried by the **separate payout-import lane** (`finance_transactions`, source `stripe_payout_import`), NOT the economic-events JEs. Comparing econ-lane impact against the FULL authoritative net double-counts the payout (~−47k AU Jan) and produces a spurious ~+42k residual. Correct residual after the POL-69 fix: **SG H1 ≈ −1,224 (reconciled); AU H1 ≈ −19,308**, ~−2.9k/month = the deferred charge-coverage gap. ~~(Unpaid Dues uncaptured + over-capture)~~ **CORRECTED (see FLOW-32/DQ-55): Unpaid Dues are NOT uncaptured** — they flow through `incidentals_invoice_paid` and settle the receivable; the real charge-side gap is orphan charges (~$3,416 AU Jan) + minor cross-lane timing. *(Gaurav+verified, 2026-07-31)*
- **FLOW-32** UNPAID-DUES SETTLEMENT MODEL (verified 2026-07-31, AU invoice `in_1SosUK...`). "Unpaid dues" is its own Sharetribe process that collects money owed on a prior trip (card failed at trip-end). The unpaid-due Stripe charge has `c.invoice IS NULL` but its `au_transactions.metadata.invoicesPaid` names the real invoice it settles. That invoice IS a normal incidentals invoice (e.g. tolls) — recognized as REVENUE at accrual via `v_AU_a_incidentals_*_invoiced` → template `incidentals_*` books **Dr 1200 (receivable) / Cr 40xx (revenue)**, line-item classified. The unpaid-due CASH is then captured by `v_AU_c_incidentals_invoice_paid` (its 2nd OR branch: `c.invoice IS NULL AND processName='unpaid-dues'`) → template `incidentals_paid` books **Dr 1019 (platform) / Cr 1200 (receivable)**. Net: revenue recognized ONCE at accrual, receivable settled at payment, no double-count — exactly the correct model. So unpaid-dues are FULLY captured and correctly hit receivables not revenue; they are NOT a coverage gap. *(Gaurav hypothesis + data-verified, 2026-07-31)*
- **DQ-55** CHARGE-COVERAGE — corrects DQ-53's stray clause. AU Jan `charge` category ($487,198 gross) partitions to: trip 238,533 (`trip_charges`✓) · distance 64,409 (`trip_distance_cash_collected`✓) · deposit 37,250✓ · verification 2,085✓ · invoice-based+unpaid-dues 141,504 (`incidentals_paid`+`subscriptions_paid`+`trip_distance_invoice_paid`, ties EXACTLY to authoritative 131,000 invoice + 10,504 unpaid-dues) · **orphans 3,416 (6 charges, `c.invoice IS NULL` + no sharetribe txn id → match NO lane = the only real charge-side gap)**. So the AU platform residual (~−2.9k/mo) is orphans + minor refund/fee cross-lane timing, NOT unpaid-dues. Orphan handling deferred (Gaurav: treat separately). *(verified, 2026-07-31)*
- **POL-70** MISC-CHARGE CATCH-ALL (AU-ONLY, 2026-07-31). AU's charge lanes left one uncaptured bucket: `charge` rows with `c.invoice IS NULL` AND no `sharetribe-transaction-id` (misc manual collections / payment-links — "Invoice XXXX" refs, `py_` payment sources). Built `v_AU_c_misc_charges_collected` (that complement, excluding deposit/verification descriptions) → template `misc_charges_collected` **Dr 1019 (platform) / Cr 4025 (Incidentals Revenue - Other)**, direct revenue (no receivable — there's no invoice behind it). This makes the AU `charge` category a VERIFIED 100% partition (real lanes + misc ties to authoritative to the cent, every H1 month). AU platform residual collapsed **−19,308 → +943 H1** (remainder = accrual-vs-cash timing, both signs, immaterial). **NOT applied to SG** — SG charge lanes already partition 100% (SG `incidentals_invoice_paid` sweeps `non_invoice_charges`; SG `trip_cash` catches unmatched-txn rows); verified SG uncaptured = 0.00/month, so a SG misc lane double-counts (+6,575 overshoot) — backed out (template + view + view_map removed). The AU-vs-SG asymmetry is real: SG lanes were built more permissively. *(Gaurav "park as incidentals other" + data-verified, 2026-07-31)*
- **POL-71** BALANCE TERMINOLOGY & RECONCILIATION GOAL (canonical, Gaurav 2026-07-31). Two balances per bank account, and reconciliation = making them equal:
  (1) **AUTHORITATIVE balance** = the external truth. For non-Stripe bank accounts it comes from the **bank statements** (newest imported `finance_transactions.running_balance`). For Stripe accounts it comes from **summing all Stripe balance-transactions** (`Σ net`, per account: platform = ex-`connect_reserved_funds`; reserve = −`Σ net(connect_reserved_funds)`). This is what the FE shows.
  (2) **COMPUTED balance** = our own books = the sum of our **POSTED** journal entries hitting that account (opening JE + economic-events JEs). Drafts are EXCLUDED (`reconciliation_service` + trial balance filter `status=POSTED`; DRAFT enters only report `basis='all'`).
  **The goal: authoritative == computed, for every account, every period.** Until prior-history (Phase B) and future (Phase C) are built, the computed balance is seeded by a **temporary `opening_balance` JE dated 1-Jan-2026 = the authoritative balance on 1-Jan**, and every month after is computed from economic events. This opening-JE convention applies UNIFORMLY to ALL bank accounts incl every Stripe account (platform, connect, deposit, reserve). *(Gaurav, 2026-07-31)*
- **DQ-56** AUTHORITATIVE Jan-1-2026 Stripe openings (from balance-txns; AU Sydney cutoff, SG UTC cutoff to match each market's monthly views): **AU platform 87,294.52 · AU reserve 4,951.07 · SG platform 44,698.52 · SG reserve 3,574.93**. Reserve opening JEs already match to the cent. **Platform opening JEs are WRONG and must be reset to authoritative: SG 1017 66,123.59→44,698.52 (−21,425.07); AU 1019 89,329.02→87,294.52 (−2,034.50).** SG discrepancy cause: the 66,123.59 opening was a manual/dashboard figure that did NOT reflect payouts already gone by Jan-1 — the balance-txns show real late-Dec SG payouts −25,000 (Dec-22), −20,000 (Dec-26), −25,000 (Dec-31), all already netted into the authoritative 44,698.52. HANDLING (POL-71): the Stripe opening is the post-payout authoritative; any payout in-transit to the external bank is a BANK-side transfer-in-transit reconciling item (Stripe→3001 AWAITING_MATCH), never an inflation of the Stripe opening. Connect (1018/1020) & Deposit (1021/1022) have NO opening JE yet — their authoritative Jan-1 needs connected-account reconstruction (the `*_balance_transactions_for_connected_accounts` table has no per-account dimension, so deposit-vs-RMS split needs the live per-account API balance minus post-Jan flow). *(verified, 2026-07-31)*
- **DQ-57** OPENINGS POSTED 2026-07-31 (source='opening_balance', dated Jan-1): SG platform 1017→44,698.52 (voided the wrong +25k in-transit amendment JE6233; +3,574.93 reserve-carve fix); AU platform 1019→87,294.52 (Sydney-midnight basis; was 89,329.02 UTC+carve); AU deposit 1022→82,253.42 (Dr 1022 / Cr 2110, from Stripe API: live 73,910.09 − net flow since Jan −8,343.33); SG 25k in-transit payout → Suspense 3300 (clears when OCBC 3001 receives it Jan-2). Reserve openings (1023/1024) already correct. **METHOD for connected-account openings (Gaurav 2026-07-31): use the Stripe API per account — `GET /v1/balance` (current) and `GET /v1/balance_transactions -H "Stripe-Account: acct_…"` summed for flow-since-Jan; opening = current − flow_since. Transfers-table alone OVERSTATES (ignores payouts/sweeps OUT).** STILL PENDING: Connect pool opening (1018/1020 = RMS+Flex++caretaker accounts, ENT-7 — same API method iterated over the pool) and SG deposit/verification (1021). NB: full-period computed balance (opening + economic-events) does NOT yet equal authoritative Jun-30 because the payout lane's transfer JEs are a separate lane still being reconciled — openings are correct; end-to-end balance recon is the next layer. *(2026-07-31)*
- **DQ-58** CONNECT-ACCOUNT <$1000 DEDUCTION RULE IS SG-ONLY (verified 2026-07-31). The "$1000 rule" (Rule 214, a transfer rule keyed by `target_bank_account_id`=SG Stripe Connect bank) books <$1000 Stripe→bank inflows as transfers that CREDIT SG connect 1018 (`categorization_engine` source, Cr 1,861.63 H1) — correctly deducting the SG connect balance without waiting for the Stripe side (we assume: if it's in the bank, it happened). **AU connect 1020 has NO such rule — only economic_events hit it (net −1,360.10).** So AU connect will NOT reconcile until an equivalent AU-scoped <$1000 connect rule is added. Confirmation for Gaurav: YES SG deducts, NO AU doesn't. *(verified, 2026-07-31)*
- **DQ-74** CONNECT-ACCOUNT RECONCILIATION MODEL — the sweep-out is booked from the BANK side, so the tie is "actual Stripe sweeps vs our <$1000-rule bookings" (Gaurav, 2026-08-02). Connect accounts (1018 SG / 1020 AU) are OUR OWN Stripe connected accounts (the rms/flexplus/caretaker pool per DQ-59), whose external bank is **OCBC-1001 or OCBC-3001 (SG) / CBA-7311 (AU)** — DISJOINT from held/deposit (1021/1022 = deposit connected accts acct_1EhuMG/acct_1JkKmQ) and reserve (1023/1024 = synthetic reserved-funds sub-ledgers). GOLDEN RULES for pool membership: (a) not held/reserve/deposit; (b) linked external bank ∈ {OCBC-1001, OCBC-3001, CBA-7311} — the DQ-59 email filter is OVER-INCLUSIVE (9 SG members actually link to host banks DBS/POSB or OCBC-2001, holding only ~535 SGD; tighten by intersecting with linked-bank). **THE MECHANISM (do not re-derive wrong):** cash DOES sweep connect→our-OCBC in H1; we book that sweep ONLY from the BANK side via the **<$1000 rule (Rule 214)** — a sub-$1000 transfer landing in OCBC posts a JE crediting the connect account (1018), on the assumption "it's in our bank, so it left connect." H1 booked only **~1,861.63** this way. **THE RECON QUESTION IS THEREFORE:** actual Stripe connect-account OUTFLOWS to our bank in H1 (query ALL outflow movements per connected account — payouts AND transfers-to-external, NOT just the `/v1/payouts` object, which reads ~0 and is the WRONG lens) **vs** what we booked via the <$1000 rule (~1,861.63). The shortfall = under-captured sweeps = the connect diff (+25,746.91 SG / +5,712.68 AU). It is NOT un-mirrored activity and NOT a plug — the fix is capturing the real sweep total the <$1000 rule misses (plus the missing AU sweep rule per DQ-58). *(Gaurav, 2026-08-02)*
  **CORRECTION (deterministic Σnet pass, 2026-08-02):** the "under-captured **sweeps**" framing above is too narrow — replace it with the ground-truth result. METHOD LESSON (the one that matters): reconcile by cumulating balance-transaction **`net`** (=amount−fee), NOT `amount`, from inception with market-local cutoffs; every earlier flip-flop came from summing `amount` or using `current−flow` drift. RESULT: connect-pool **openings are essentially RIGHT** (AU 8,333.22 exact; SG re-derives to **30,175.93** vs the 31,225.86 booked in DQ-59 — a −1,049.93 opening tweak, not a 4-9× error). The entire error is on the **CLOSING side = under-captured H1 OUTFLOW**, and it is **refund-heavy**, not sweep-only: the deterministic H1 net movement is **SG −17,944.65 / AU −7,072.78**, of which the <$1000 rule booked only ~1,861.63 (SG) / ~−1,360.10 econ-events (AU). So the diff decomposes into (a) real bank sweeps the <$1000 rule missed + the absent AU sweep rule (DQ-58) AND (b) guest-refund contra outflow — the type-split (bank-sweep vs guest-refund) was the pending decomposition when the exercise was PARKED. DEFERRED FIX (see STATUS ▶ Stripe Connect — PARKED): type-split → book catch-up JEs (AU +5,712.68, SG +~24,037.74 with the −1,049.93 SG opening tweak) → add the AU <$1000 sweep rule (DQ-58) → re-derive DQ-59 SG opening to 30,175.93 → tighten the pool filter to linked-bank ∈ {OCBC-1001, OCBC-3001, CBA-7311}, dropping the 9 mis-tagged host accounts. *(deterministic, 2026-08-02)*
- **FLOW-33** DEPOSIT SWEEP correct booking (Gaurav 2026-07-31, corrects FLOW-13's "Cr 2110"). When cash is swept from the Stripe deposit connect account (`acct_1JkKmQ`) to our own bank (CBA), it is a MOVE OF OUR CASH between two asset accounts — book **Dr bank (1015) / Cr deposit account (1022)**. The liability to guests (2110) is UNCHANGED (we still owe them; only the cash location moved). ~~FLOW-13 said Cr 2110~~ — that wrongly reduced the liability. FIX: AU Mar-27 sweep 42,000 (JE6246) reclassified via JE8771 (Dr 2110 / Cr 1022) so the deposit account 1022 reduces on the sweep. These bank↔deposit sweeps are straightforward 1:1 matches (no rule needed) — match each such bank inflow against the deposit account. *(Gaurav, 2026-07-31)*
- **DQ-59** CONNECT POOL identification for the opening: email patterns are NOISY (AU `%drivemate%` = 19 incl external hosts like dicksondrivemate01 / engageindustries; SG = 128). Per ENT-7 the definitive signal is the LINKED BANK (AU CBA-7311 / SG OCBC-1001, in `connected_accounts.settings`), excluding the deposit + verification accounts. AU pool = 12 accts → opening **8,333.22** (only rms4/5/6 hold cash); SG pool = 95 accts → opening **31,225.86** (34 nonzero; 1 acct acct_1P1hDS returned 403-forbidden, excluded — small). Posted Dr 1020/1018 / Cr 3200. *(2026-07-31)*

- **DQ-61** PLATFORM END-TO-END TIE (computed vs authoritative Jun-30) — DONE both markets 2026-07-31. AU +943.24, SG −1,224.44 (both = the immaterial accrual-vs-cash charge-timing residual, wash out over full period). SG needed a fix: the 25k in-transit payout was DOUBLE-deducted — once by matching the authoritative Jan-1 opening (which already excludes it, gone Dec-31) and again when it landed OCBC 1016 on Jan-2 (JE1739 categorized as Cr 1017 platform). Fix JE8774: Dr 1017 / Cr 3300 25,000 — the Jan-2 bank arrival clears the Suspense (in-transit), does NOT re-deduct the platform. Suspense 3300 now nets to 0.00. **GENERAL PRINCIPLE: a payout that left Stripe pre-Jan-1 but lands in our bank post-Jan-1 must clear the in-transit Suspense on arrival, NOT credit the Stripe account (which already excluded it via the opening) — else double-deduction.** Payout lane (categorization_engine, Stripe→bank sweeps) otherwise reconciles: SG authoritative payout H1 −567,045.10 matched our −592,045.10 minus exactly the 25k. SG verification acct (1021) opening = 0.00 (empty at Jan-1; its current 15,488.80 all accrued in 2026 — separate wiring gap, not an opening). *(verified, 2026-07-31)*
- **DQ-54** The two Stripe Platform reconciliation lanes are independent and must not be conflated: (1) **economic-events lane** = view-derived accrual/cash JEs (`source='economic_events'`), fully reproducible — safe to delete + re-stage + re-project per period; (2) **payout-import lane** = actual cash-out bank transactions (`finance_transactions`, `source='stripe_payout_import'`), deduped by Stripe balance_transaction_id, idempotent. Re-deriving the economic-events lane (delete H1 JEs+events → stage_month → project_month) leaves the payout lane untouched. Both together sum to the true platform balance change. *(verified, 2026-07-31)*
- **DQ-62** VENDOR-INVOICE SOURCES & the Drive↔Retool naming convention. Vendor invoices reach `finance_invoices` from two sources sharing the SAME underlying files: (1) **Retool** (`uploaded_by='ingest:retool_invoice'`, 1,342 rows) and (2) a **Google Drive vendor-invoice folder** (`1XJ6H_D1QVrnGyOS-vPGRG2tslCbUR_E2`, flat, 0 subfolders, **1,406 files** as of 2026-07-31, mostly owner `zilla@drivelah.sg`). Retool renames on upload: a Drive file `Invoice_16833618_160426090658.pdf` becomes `pdf_s3_key = invoices/entity_X/YYYY/MM/{12charHash}_{RetoolTimestamp}-{OriginalName}` (e.g. `9693ec224a73_1777268132695-Invoice_16833618_160426090658.pdf`). So to dedup Drive filenames against the loaded corpus by NAME: from each `pdf_s3_key` basename take everything after the first `_`, then strip a leading `\d{13,16}-` Retool timestamp, lowercase, compare to the Drive title (also strip that prefix in case the Drive copy already carries it). Filename-match is only a cheap pre-filter — the AUTHORITATIVE dedup is SHA-256 `pdf_content_hash` (identical file already loaded → skip) plus the DB semantic unique index (same counterparty+invoice_number+entity → duplicate). Drive strays load as `uploaded_by='ingest:gdrive'`, `status='draft'` via `documentation/wip/ingest_drive_invoices.py`; manifest at `documentation/wip/VENDOR_DRIVE_MANIFEST.csv`. *(2026-07-31)*
- **DQ-63** The Drive folder is NOT a pure invoice folder — ~55 of 1,406 files are genuine non-invoices the ingest gate correctly rejects (`document_type` ∈ statement/letter/report/spreadsheet_screenshot or no amount+date): SOA statements, ATO overdue-super notices, TP EXCESS RECOVERY LETTERs, Excess Requests, PROOF-OF-LOSS docs, Superannuation accruals, Claim Acknowledgements, Grab receipts, LODs. Separately, a truncated/incomplete DOWNLOAD produces the same `not_invoice`/`document_type=other` gate result (no extractable amount/date) even for a real invoice — so an invoice-named file gated as `other` is a download-integrity red flag, not necessarily a true non-invoice. Guard: after download verify size >~2KB and `%PDF` header before ingest. *(2026-07-31)*

- **POL-72** COUNTERPARTY-MATCHING PRINCIPLES (Gaurav 2026-07-31). TWO distinct matchers, two candidate pools:
  ① **Invoice ingestion** (`ingest_*_invoices.py`, `VendorMatchingService.match_or_create`): **fuzzy FIRST → Haiku fallback if fuzzy fails** (threshold 0.80/0.90; `fuzzy_match_vendor` then `llm_match_vendor`). Candidate pool = **ALL type='vendor' counterparties, active OR inactive** (drop the old `status='active'` filter — an inactive vendor is the same entity). Extraction is LLM (`ai_extraction_service`); matching is fuzzy-then-Haiku.
  ② **Reconciliation / stray-invoice cleanup**: **Haiku-led**, candidate pool = **ALL counterparties, every type AND status** (vendor/employee/investor/government/…, active+inactive). No type/status filter at all.
  ROOT CAUSE it fixes (DQ-63): the old matchers ran against `type='vendor' AND status='active'` (468/632), so existing counterparties that were inactive vendors, or typed employee/government/etc., or created after a stale candidate snapshot, were flagged NEW_VENDOR. Re-running the recon over all 632 resolved **47 of 50** previously-"new" vendors (e.g. Bytchenkov id49 inactive, Craig Letters id163 employee, Inland Revenue id550 government, + the 695-737 batch the stale pool missed). Fixes applied to `vendor_matching_service.match_or_create` + both ingest scripts (fuzzy→Haiku + all-vendor pool). Corrected list: `documentation/wip/QUARANTINED_VENDORS_HAIKU_v2.csv`. *(Gaurav, 2026-07-31)*

- **POL-73** INVOICE DUPLICATE POLICY + FULL-INGEST FOR REVIEW (Gaurav 2026-07-31). The DB is the source of truth and everything lands `draft` (nothing books until approved), so ALL Retool rows are ingested — including duplicates/not-invoices/no-doc — so the team can review the complete picture in the dashboard (existing buttons only: draft→void, pending→reject/approve; no new stages/columns). DUPLICATE RULE — "first one wins": migration 046 made `uq_finance_invoices_semantic` an **active-only** partial index (`WHERE … status NOT IN ('draft','void','rejected')`), so duplicate DRAFTs upload freely but a second copy **cannot be promoted to pending_approval+** (DB blocks it). Duplicate marker lives in `ai_extraction_raw.recon.duplicate = {is_duplicate, duplicate_of, reason}` (shown next to the existing `vendor_flag`, no new column). Detection = pdf_content_hash (exact) + semantic key (vendor+invoice#+date+currency). BACKFILL 2026-07-31: source `/tmp/vendor_all.json` was a temp file, gone; reconstructed not-ingested rows from `VENDOR_INGEST_MANIFEST.csv` as draft stubs (`uploaded_by='ingest:retool_backfill'`, `recon.stub=true`, `date_unknown=true` sentinel 1900-01-01, provisional_paid from retool_status='Closed', counterparty fuzzy-matched). NB manifest is APPEND-MODE (each invoice logged per run w/ that run's outcome) — first pass keyed dedup on outcome and over-created 531 (163 duplicated already-uploaded invoices), those 163 deleted. FINAL 368 genuine stubs (no_attachment 135, no_file 88, not_invoice 84, duplicate 61). By source: retool_invoice 1342 + retool_backfill 368 (retool 1710 ≈ 1755 distinct) + gdrive 188 = 1898 total; 61 duplicate-flagged. REMAINING: FE review-queue display (duplicate/stub/outcome badges) + change the live ingest to insert future duplicates as draft-flagged (not skip). *(Gaurav, 2026-07-31)*

- **POL-74** INVOICE REVIEW & POSTING WORKFLOW (Gaurav 2026-07-31). Lifecycle: draft (review queue) → pending_approval → approved → paid; + rejected/void. RULES enforced backend-side: (1) NO direct-to-approved — `approve()` only from pending_approval; `submit()` never auto-approves (approval rules disabled) → everything lands in pending_approval. (2) HARD mandate to leave draft: entity + counterparty + contra_account_code (COA) + a REAL invoice_date (the 1900-01-01 stub sentinel is blocked) + amount(NOT NULL). (3) SOFT block: submitting a doc flagged `not_invoice` requires an `override_reason` (may proceed WITH a reason, stored). (4) FULL AUDIT (migration 047): who/when for submit/approve/reject/void + rejection_reason/void_reason/submit_override_reason; exposed via API `audit` block. `uploaded_by` must be the real user for UI uploads (system tag only for bulk ingests). POSTING date = **invoice_date** (never upload date): Dr expense / Cr AP(2000) at invoice_date; a MULTI-MONTH service_period → Dr Prepaid(**1300**, corrected per DQ-67; the 1200 here was the pre-renumber code)/Cr AP + amortization schedule spread over the service months (released monthly per the DA section). COA endpoint was crashing on a stray `status='INACTIVE'` row (enum is ACTIVE/SUSPENDED) → fixed. Coverage: 1530/1898 have real invoice_date, 368 stubs = 1900 sentinel (can't post until dated); 1261 have service periods. *(Gaurav, 2026-07-31)*

- **POL-82** BANK-STATEMENT GAP-FILL SOURCING POLICY (Gaurav 2026-08-02). For the vendor payment reconciliation (§2.7) we must load ALL bank txns back to each account's inception. Where we lack the actual statement, source the gap by this rule, PER ACCOUNT: **old pre-2023 gaps → take the BANK TRANSACTIONS as recorded in QuickBooks (the bank-section txn lines, NOT the journal entries)**; **2025-and-beyond gaps → Gaurav supplies the real statements.** Specifics: OCBC-1001 fill Jun-2019→Jan-2020 from QB bank txns; OCBC-3001 fill all 2019/2020/2021/2022 gaps from QB bank txns; CBA fill 2021/2022 gaps from QB; DBS has NO historical gap (see DQ-75). All four: 2025+ from Gaurav's statements. Authoritative account-open anchors: OCBC (both) Jun-2019, CBA Aug-2021, DBS Jun-2022. Coverage artifact: `wip/statement_coverage/coverage_ALL.html` (green=have · amber=fill-from-QB · red=await-statement). *(Gaurav, 2026-08-02)*
- **POL-83** BULK PROD-DB LOAD SAFETY POLICY (VR-1c incident, 2026-08-02). Any bulk write to the shared prod finance DB (`finance_transactions`, collections-db) MUST: (1) run **FOREGROUND + SUPERVISED**, one account/source at a time, operator watching the row count — NEVER a detached background agent; (2) hold a **single-run LOCK** — no concurrent copies, no retry loop that relaunches on crash; (3) map every row to a **VERIFIED `bank_account_id`** and REFUSE on ambiguity (no catch-all fallback); (4) be **memory-safe** (stream/batch inserts, don't hold the whole corpus); (5) **fingerprint-dedup + idempotent**; (6) **RAW ingest only** (`auto_categorize=False` → IMPORTED, zero JEs) with `finance_journal_entries`/`_lines` counts guarded as a tripwire; (7) **pre-load backup** (`COPY finance_transactions TO CSV`) + `finance_sync_runs` receipts for reversibility; (8) a **PLAUSIBILITY gate** beyond balance-chain (rows-per-statement sanity + duplicate detection) — chain-reconcile proves the math CLOSES, not that rows are unique/real. After running, **verify the DB directly; never trust an agent's "done."** Incident: a detached loader OOM'd on large files, mis-mapped ALL OCBC to catch-all acct id1, and got auto-respawned, polluting `finance_transactions` with ~13–22k pre-2026 IMPORTED rows TWICE; JE/JL never moved (4420/8854); cleaned via backup + delete-by-not-in-backup + a pre-2026-non-reconciled purge. *(Gaurav+incident, 2026-08-02)*
- **POL-84** VENDOR AP RECONCILIATION — UN-INVOICED PAYMENTS ARE DIRECT EXPENSES, NEVER AP (Gaurav 2026-08-02; **EVOLVING** — refine as we walk real vendors). We never had complete invoice capture: for many vendors/windows there are payments with NO invoice (never captured, or predate the invoice mechanism), some vendors have a few invoices not all, and a vendor's invoices often start mid-history. **THE PRINCIPLE: only invoiced obligations flow through AP (2000); a payment with NO matching invoice is booked as a DIRECT EXPENSE (Dr expense / Cr bank), never as an AP credit.** Consequence: AP can never go negative from un-invoiced payments, so a vendor can NEVER show a false OVERPAYMENT. **Vendor outstanding = Σ(open, unmatched invoices we actually hold); always ≥ 0.** Keep TWO distinct numbers per vendor, never conflate: (1) **AP / invoice outstanding** = open invoices (what we still owe on invoices we know about); (2) **total cash paid** = all payments (relationship/cash view, NOT outstanding). MATCHING DECISION TREE: (a) payment clearly maps to ONE invoice (amount+date, ideally a reference) → knock off `Dr AP / Cr bank`; (b) ambiguous (could be several invoices) → don't guess — review, or oldest-invoice-first; (c) payment matches NO open invoice → **direct expense** (not AP, not overpayment). WORKED EXAMPLE (Tokio Marine): early payments with no invoice = direct expense; later invoices = AP accrual (`Dr expense / Cr AP`); payments against them = knock-off; outstanding = the later invoices not yet matched — the early un-invoiced payments sit as expenses and never distort it. OPEN POLICY to settle per-vendor as we evolve: **STRICT** amount-match (unmatched payment → direct expense; conservative, may slightly overstate owed) vs **FIFO-on-account** (unmatched payment to a vendor with open invoices pays down the oldest; tracks net cash but risks settling an invoice with a payment meant for something else). *(Gaurav, 2026-08-02 — evolving)*
- **DQ-77** OCBC BANK-ACCOUNT IDENTITY AMBIGUITY. `finance_bank_accounts` has NO clean "OCBC 1001" / "OCBC 3001" rows — only **id=1 "OCBC Main account" (coa 1000, SGD)** and **id=18 "OCBC" (coa 1016, SGD)**.
