This unblocks drafting for a mixed USD/INR/SGD/BDT entity with NO FX rates on file (it used to hard-fail on a missing rate at draft). **FX runs ONLY at submit**, when the DRAFT JE is built (`hr_payroll_service.set_functional_totals` — the one conversion site — rolls each payslip into the entity functional at the run date and fills the run totals; `submit_for_approval` builds a DRAFT JE, `submit_run` posts). **JE date = the run date** (15th mid-month / 27th end-of-month), already correct via `_derive_cycle_dates`. **VOID:** `void_run` + `POST /payroll-runs/<id>/void` reverses the run's JE and marks it VOID so the cycle can be re-run; refused once payouts are in the register (payment in flight); audited. **Names:** `get_run_items` now returns `employee_name` (from `users.name`) — the FE draft used to show the raw `hr_employees.id`. Clone-verified via HTTP on the real 40-employee entity. NEVER prod. *(Gaurav+Pickle, 2026-08-16)*
- **DQ-110** PAYROLL DEDUCTIONS TODAY = A BLANKET SG CPF ON EVERYONE, CAP APPLIED IN NATIVE CCY (LIKELY WRONG DATA) (Pickle, 2026-08-16). As seeded on the clone, 45 of 48 employees carry exactly TWO deduction rules: `CPF_EMPLOYEE` 20% (employee-bears, reduces net, Cr 2300) and `CPF_EMPLOYER` 17% (employer, Dr expense / Cr 2300), BOTH with `ordinary_wage_cap=6000`. Net = gross − employee-borne deductions, so net = gross − min(gross,6000)×0.20. **Two problems:** (1) CPF is SINGAPORE-only, but it's applied to INR/BDT/USD offshore staff (Ankish Mody, Rahul Moitra, etc.) who are not CPF members; (2) the 6000 ordinary-wage cap is an SGD ceiling but `_calculate_deduction` applies it in the salary's OWN currency, so an INR 187,000 salary gets CPF on min(187000,6000)=6000 **INR** → a nonsensical 1200 INR deduction. So the gross/net gap the FE shows is arithmetically consistent but built on deduction config that does not reflect reality. Deduction model + correct per-jurisdiction setup still to be decided with Gaurav. *(Pickle, 2026-08-16)*
- **DQ-111** The 2019 bounced Journey Horizon TT left S$468.93 of FX/bank-fee residue INSIDE the 1710 Technology Development capitalized base (out 16,042.15 Nov-29, returned 15,573.22 Dec-24 — the difference is fees+FX, not development spend). The draft asset register carries it as asset #3's remaining base amortizing over 36 months; PENDING Gaurav ruling whether to expense it instead. (found via asset-register draft, 2026-08-17)
- **DQ-112** Vendor portals (AFCA proven, 2026-08-17) REGENERATE the invoice PDF on every download — visually identical files, DIFFERENT bytes/sha256. Byte-hash dedup therefore can NEVER be sufficient for portal-downloaded invoices; the semantic layer (same vendor + invoice number [+ amount]) is the real duplicate gate and must hard-block (Gaurav ruling 2026-08-17: duplicate warning is NOT advisory). Verified: the re-downloaded AFCA 2548322 copy hashed e649f9b1… vs the original 9a57d792…, and the semantic detector returned action=block/confidence 1.0 against invoice #2514. (Gaurav live-test, 2026-08-17)
- **POL-149** PAYROLL DEDUCTIONS ARE MANUAL FOR NOW; ONBOARDING NO LONGER AUTO-APPLIES (Gaurav, 2026-08-16). Decision: the team enters each employee's deductions **manually** (per entity + whether we handle their tax); auto-calculation comes later. Origin of the bad data (DQ-110) was `hr_onboarding_service` auto-applying `REGION_DEFAULT_DEDUCTIONS[entity.country]` and ignoring `tax_treatment`. **Changes:** (1) onboarding no longer auto-applies region defaults — a new hire starts with ZERO deductions (explicit `default_deductions` payload still honored); (2) the pre-existing auto-seeded rules were CLEARED on the clone (90 rows), so a draft now shows net==gross until deductions are entered; (3) `DEDUCTION_COA` (a hardcoded dict in `hr_onboarding_service.py`, **NOT a DB table**) had wrong payable codes — super pointed at 2310 and income-tax at 2320, neither exists; fixed to 2302 (super) and 2305 (income tax), added PAYG_WITHHOLDING→2301; (4) `add_deduction_rule` now DERIVES coa_debit/coa_credit/employee_bears/cap from the deduction type, so the team supplies only type + rate + effective date; (5) added `delete_deduction_rule` + `DELETE /employees/<id>/deduction-rules/<id>`; (6) FE: a manual **Deductions editor** in the employee drawer (list/add/remove; % entered as a whole number, sent as a fraction). **Statutory reference for the future auto-calc** (rates flagged, not yet built): SG CPF employee 20% / employer 17% capped at the OW ceiling (SGD 8,000 in 2026, code still says 6,000); AU super 12% from 1 Jul 2025 (code said 11.5%); AU PAYG withholding is progressive per ATO tables, not a flat rate. Latent mismatch to revisit: FE `tax_treatment` values are `EMPLOYER_WITHHOLD` / `SELF_MANAGED`, but `payroll_service.fan_out_to_register` gates statutory payouts on `tax_treatment=='internal'` — three different vocabularies for one flag. Clone-tested via HTTP. *(Gaurav+Pickle, 2026-08-16)*
- **POL-150** FX RATES: SOURCE + THE RECURRING LOADER (Gaurav+Pickle, 2026-08-16). Rates live in **our** `finance_fx_rates` table (year_month, from, to, rate, **source**, created_at); the read path (`fx_service.get_monthly_rate`) inverts, so one direction covers both. Source is **ECB via the Frankfurter API** (`api.frankfurter.dev/v1/{YYYY-MM-01}` → nearest ECB business day ≤ that date; `source='ECB/frankfurter <date>'`). **The gap that was there:** the table was backfilled ONCE by hand (84 months through 2026-07, all inserted 2026-08-04) with **no recurring job** — so 2026-08 and every future month was empty and every foreign JE refused to post (POL-26). **Built the mechanism** (`fx_loader_service` + `/api/finance/fx-rates/{load,status}` + `scripts/load_fx_rates.py` cron entrypoint + FE "FX Rates" tab). **Required-pairs rule (Gaurav):** for every ENTITY functional currency (SGD, AUD) and every OPERATING currency we hold value in (salary currencies + bank-account currencies + entity currencies), store both directions. Operating set on the data today: {SGD, AUD, USD, INR, BDT, EUR, MYR, PKR}. **ECB coverage gap:** Frankfurter covers ~30 ECB currencies but NOT **BDT** (we have BDT salaries) or **PKR** (a bank-account currency) — the loader flags these `unsupported`; finance enters them manually (route stores the inverse too, `source='manual'`). The cron script exits non-zero while any required pair is still missing, so a monitor alerts. Clone-verified end-to-end (FE→bff→api): 2026-08 loaded 20 real ECB pairs + manual BDT/PKR → status complete 26/26. Placeholder BDT/PKR values on the clone are `source='manual'` and must be replaced with real rates. *(Gaurav+Pickle, 2026-08-16)*
- **POL-151** D&A PLACEMENT (accounting-principles ruling, Pickle under Gaurav delegation, 2026-08-17): depreciation & amortisation of OPERATING assets is an OPERATING expense, presented above operating income (IFRS/SFRS; it is the D&A in EBITDA) — never in below-the-line Other Expense, which is reserved for non-operating items (FX, disposals, one-offs). Function split: depreciation of assets used to DELIVER the service (fleet vehicles, on-ground equipment) → Cost of Sales; amortisation of platform/tech development + depreciation of office/admin assets → Operating Expenses under sub-category 'Depreciation & Amortisation'. Applied: 7400 Amortisation - Technology Development recategorized Other Expense → Operating Expenses/D&A (clone; prod at config apply). (Pickle ruling, 2026-08-17)
- **DQ-108** SURGICAL FX PASS — DONE vs GATED (Pickle, 2026-08-16). The POL-141 FX fixes were done SURGICALLY (not the full posting-service rebuild — Gaurav's call). **Shipped + clone-tested + committed** on `260814_payout_module`: `fx_service.to_functional_or_same` (shared caller-converts helper); **F1/F2** claim bill + reimbursement convert to entity functional, residue→7100 (58569ea); **F5** cross-entity allocation books each entity in ITS functional ccy independently (ccc17cc); **F3** payroll accrual converts each payslip (mixed USD/INR) to entity functional before aggregating (ac047b8); **G1** journal_service.create invariant rejects a line DECLARING a foreign ccy without native+valid rate (621e7c7); conftest model-import fix (09111b8). Each verified by a Postgres-clone script in `tests/clone_fx/` (NEVER prod — `.env` defaults to prod RDS `collections-db`, so DATABASE_URL is pinned to `finance_local`). **GATED / not done — need decisions:** **F4** (cross-entity payroll payment FX) + **P3** (retire old payroll knockoff) are interdependent and hinge on a BUSINESS FACT — the NEW register knockoff (`:884`) is SAME-ENTITY only; the OLD `_try_payroll_knockoff` (`:910`→`create_payroll_payment_entries`) is the ONLY cross-entity payroll settlement path. So: does Drive lah ever pay one entity's payroll from another entity's bank? If NO → retire old (P3), F4 moot. If YES → keep + fix F4, register model needs cross-entity support. **P1** (delete test-only `payroll_service.create_run`) needs migrating 8 tests to `hr_payroll_service.create_run` (different behaviour). **P2** (retire immediate-post `hr_payroll_service.submit_run` vs approval-gated `submit_for_approval`) = route rewiring. **IC1** (intercompany independent per-entity booking) changes the pairing DISPATCH (sensitive matching area) — biggest behaviour change, deserves its own focused pass. **HARNESS BUG FOUND (pre-existing, not the FX work):** the SQLite in-memory unit suite is BROKEN for every DB-fixture test — `hr_employees.teams` is a Postgres ARRAY column that SQLite `create_all` can't render (`UnsupportedCompilationError: ARRAY`). Only non-DB tests + the Postgres integration tests run. Fix = a SQLite-compat type (JSON fallback) for the ARRAY columns. *(Pickle, 2026-08-16)*
- **DQ-107** EXHAUSTIVE JE-POSTING SCAN CLOSED — 2 more paths found, surface now complete (Pickle, 2026-08-16). Full-codebase scan (all `FinanceJournalEntry(`/`FinanceJournalLine(`, `journal_service.create/post_entry`, direct `status=POSTED`, raw SQL, bulk, migrations, void/reversal, dup/merge). **Two not pinned before:** (a) **`transaction_service.approve` (:214) is the real DRAFT→POSTED trigger** for bank-driven JEs — flips a matched txn's linked JE to POSTED DIRECTLY (`je.status = POSTED`, bypassing `post_entry`) and posts all MATCHED partner legs of that JE at once; this is the "downstream" post trigger the categorization DRAFT lanes rely on. (b) **Stripe Sync is 2 files** — `sync_service.py` + `journal_entry_builder.py` (`build_je`/`build_payout_je`, status=POSTED); still dormant. **Ruled OUT (not posting paths):** migrations only create/alter schema (never INSERT JE data); zero raw `INSERT INTO finance_journal_*`; zero `bulk_insert_mappings`/`add_all` for JEs; no recurring/duplicate/merge JE; `void_entry` is soft-VOID (status=VOID, no reversing entry); `csv_adapters`/`account_service`/`bank_account_service` don't post. **COMPLETE inventory — Create:** journal_service.create (23) · economic_events.project_month (direct) · stripe_sync (2 files, direct, dormant) · 5 vr2_post_* scripts. **Post:** post_entry (manual route + payroll decide_group) · transaction_service.approve (direct flip) · 8 inline status=POSTED. **Void:** void_entry + invoice_service.void (soft). *(Pickle, 2026-08-16)*
- **DQ-105** DBS-USD IS AN EXTERNAL-PAYMENT ACCOUNT, NOT AN INTERNAL-FX CONDUIT; DBS↔OCBC IS CROSS-ENTITY (Pickle, 2026-08-15). On the clone, DBS accounts (USD 13, SGD 15, EUR 16) are **entity 1**; OCBC (Main 1, +18) are **entity 2**. So a "DBS USD → OCBC" transfer is **cross-entity (intercompany)**, not intra-entity. DBS-USD outflows are almost all **OUTWARD TELEGRAPHIC TRANSFERs to external vendors** (−50k/−60k/−100k/−250k USD), NOT internal conversions into DBS-SGD; DBS-SGD inflows tagged "INWARD TELEGRAPHIC TRANSFER" are inbound remittances from OUTSIDE, not the landing leg of a DBS-USD move. Consequence: the data holds **no clean historic intra-entity cross-currency internal-transfer pair** to regression-test POL-144 against — the fix was verified with a realistic synthetic pair exercising the real code paths instead. *(Pickle, 2026-08-15)*
- **POL-143** REBASE 2026-08-15: invoice currency FIXED by merged main; my machines still open (Pickle). Rebased `260814_payout_module` onto origin/main (finance-api 36 commits over 61, ZERO conflicts; bff 5, ac 12; all compile/tsc-clean/boot). **Merged main RESOLVES the POL-141 INVOICE gaps** (`:743/1450/1486/1511`): the reference pattern is `approve()` → `fx_service.to_functional(total_native, inv_ccy, func_ccy, invoice.invoice_date)` (invoice-DATE rate) stamping currency/native_amount/fx_rate per line; `post_pairing` Mechanism A functional bill; `create_ap_payment_entries` converts bank-native at PAYMENT date + clears functional AP residue to **7100** (auto-FX). Manual JE route + amortization made functional-safe too. **STILL OPEN (POL-141, MY machines, to fix with this same pattern):** claim bill `claim_service.approve` + reimbursement `create_claim_payment_entries`; payroll accrual `_build_je_lines_and_groups`/`submit_run`, cross-entity `payroll_service.py:513/549`; categorization `_try_claim_knockoff` + `_try_payroll_register_knockoff` + transfer/IC/allocation paths (2015/2073/2081/2161/2171). Apply per-entity/per-leg conversion at JE creation; retire `amount_sgd` shadow (POL-142). *(Pickle, 2026-08-15)*

## Depreciation, Amortization & Prepaid Release (DA-n) — LOCKED DESIGN 2026-08-17

> Design ratified by Gaurav 2026-08-17 in conversation. This section REPLACES all earlier DA notes
> (the pre-2026-08-17 DA-1..DA-9 draft entries are superseded and were removed).

- **DA-1 ONE MECHANISM, ONE VERB.** Depreciation (tangibles: vehicles, computers, in-car devices,
  furniture) and amortization (intangibles: capitalized development, brand) are the SAME treatment —
  spread a cost across the months it serves. Prepaid release (insurance, annual SaaS bought for a
  future period) is the same verb again. All three are served by ONE service (`amortization_service`)
  and ONE run endpoint: `POST /api/finance/amortization/run`. Differences are inputs only (life,
  account pair), never method. The nouns survive for disclosure/tax, not mechanics.

- **DA-2 PLAN vs ENTRIES (the core ruling).** The PLAN is created up-front — an asset register row at
  capitalization, a prepaid schedule row at invoice approval. The JOURNAL ENTRIES are born monthly,
  and ONLY for months that have ARRIVED. Never pre-create future-dated entries: the ledger must only
  ever contain what is true today; future rows would pollute as-at reports, must be hunted down on any
  change, and can disturb closed periods. Future visibility belongs on a SCREEN (projection), not in
  the ledger.

- **DA-3 TRIGGER = MONTH-LOCK.** The run is part of the month-end checklist: run the cycle (assets +
  prepaids together, one call), verify, then close the month. Auto-registration still happens at
  posting time (a debit into a policy-covered asset account creates its register row via
  `check_and_create_schedule`, wired into transaction approval).

- **DA-4 CATCH-UP IS SAFE AND DATE-CORRECT.** Miss August, run in September: the engine posts BOTH,
  each dated to its own month (Aug charge in Aug, Sep charge in Sep). Monthly reporting stays right.
  Caveat: a charge landing in an already-CLOSED month reopens it — hence DA-3's order (post, then lock).

- **DA-5 CHANGES ARE PROSPECTIVE, NEVER RETROSPECTIVE.** If the thing changes mid-life (asset sold or
  written down, prepaid corrected): nothing posted yet → just fix the plan; months already posted →
  reduce the remaining base and RECOMPUTE the monthly amount over the months still to run; history is
  never rewritten. A wholesale error (duplicate invoice, wrong amount) is a CORRECTION instead: reverse
  and rebuild. Closed periods stay untouched at the cost of the monthly figure changing mid-life.

- **DA-6 ONE VIEW.** Assets and prepaids appear on ONE screen answering one question: *what have we
  paid for that hasn't hit the P&L yet, and when will it?* Each row: what it is, cost, released so far,
  remaining, monthly rate, plan runway. Type column/filter separates assets from prepaids. Display
  nuance: an asset shows COST and ACCUMULATED separately (cost never moves — a contra account carries
  the wear); a prepaid shows one balance draining to zero.

- **DA-7 THE MECHANICS.** Asset monthly JE: `Dr <expense> / Cr <accumulated contra>`; the asset account
  itself never moves after capitalization. Prepaid monthly JE: `Dr <expense> / Cr 1300 Prepayments` —
  the prepaid asset drains to zero. Both idempotent via a months-posted cursor; both true-up the final
  month so rounding never leaves a residue.

- **DA-8 THE RULEBOOK — LOCKED (Gaurav owns these lives, NOT Kaveesh; ratified 2026-08-17).**
  1710 Technology Development → 36mo → 1810 / 7400 · 1700 Brand → 60mo → 1800 / 7400 ·
  1500 Computer & Peripherals → 36mo → 1590 / 7300 · 1510 Hardware Devices → 36mo → 1591 / 7301 ·
  1520 Motor Vehicles → 60mo → 1592 / 7302 · 1530 Furniture & Fixtures → 60mo → 1593 / 7303.
  Accounts 1590–1593 (accumulated depreciation contras) and 7302/7303 were CREATED 2026-08-17 — none
  existed for tangibles.

- **DA-9 PLACEMENT (= POL-151).** D&A of operating assets is an OPERATING expense above operating
  income (it is the D&A in EBITDA); below-the-line Other Expense is for non-operating items only.
  Assets that DELIVER the service (fleet vehicles 7302, in-car devices 7301) depreciate into COST OF
  SALES; platform amortisation (7400) and office assets (7300, 7303) sit in Operating Expenses under
  sub-category 'Depreciation & Amortisation'.

- **DA-10 THE DATA MODEL.** `finance_coa_amortization_policies` = the rulebook (one row per asset class;
  a life change touches ONE row and defines what the inspector checks coverage against).
  `finance_asset_schedules` = the asset register (base after adjustments, months_posted cursor, status).
  `finance_amortization_schedules` = prepaid spreads (invoice-born; drains, no life events).
  Journal links: `finance_journal_entries.source_schedule_id` → asset schedule;
  `.source_prepaid_schedule_id` → prepaid schedule (added 2026-08-17; the first FKs the asset table).

- **DA-11 STATE 2026-08-17 (clone-verified).** Prepaid release engine BUILT into the native service
  (`run_prepaids`) and catch-up run on the clone: 664 monthly releases, 0 errors, S$1,325,855 moved
  from 1300 Prepayments into the P&L (2023 19,102 · 2024 339,093 · 2025 771,008 · 2026 196,652),
  leaving S$32,942 correctly still parked in live schedules. Asset side is seeded but idle (register
  empty: history-year assets never passed through approval — needs the backfill). Engine hardened to
  commit PER SCHEDULE after a first-run failure orphaned journals and caused a double-release on retry.

- **DA-13 THE SCHEDULED-POSTINGS ENGINE (Gaurav, 2026-08-17).** D&A + prepaid release is an ENGINE in
  its own right, a SIBLING of the categorization engine: categorization turns BANK TRANSACTIONS into
  journals; this one turns SCHEDULES into journals. One entry point — `amortization_service.run_all`
  (route: `POST /api/finance/amortization/run`) — executes every pass in order: (1) mid-life asset
  adjustments, (2) asset depreciation/amortisation, (3) prepaid releases; over everything pending,
  idempotently, posting only ARRIVED months. Called at month-lock (DA-3), from the UI button, and
  inside each recon year pass. Same discipline as the categorization engine: one call clears all
  outstanding work, safe to re-run, catch-up correct.

- **DA-14 PREPAID AND CAPITALIZED ARE MUTUALLY EXCLUSIVE — THE ACCOUNT PICKS THE ROUTE (Gaurav,
  2026-08-17).** A cost is either *waiting to become* an expense or it is *already* an asset; it can
  never be both. Prepaid route: park in 1300 Prepayments, release monthly into the P&L, governed by
  the **service period**. Capitalized route: park in the asset account, amortize monthly through the
  asset register, governed by the **policy's useful life**. So the chosen chart-of-accounts code
  decides the route, and a service period is meaningful ONLY on an EXPENSE / COST_OF_SALES account —
  on an asset or liability account it is **ignored** (Gaurav: "if it's already booked into an asset,
  you can't also rebook it into prepayment"). The invoice's COA choice is never overridden or lost:
  the schedule *stores* it as `expense_account_code` and each monthly release debits exactly the
  account that was picked; only the approval debit is swapped to 1300. Enforced in three places:
  `invoice_service` ignores the period on a non-P&L account, `amortization_service.run_prepaids`
  refuses to release into one, and INSP-12 flags any that already exist. Why it matters: spreading
  into an asset shuffles money between two assets and never reaches the P&L while the register
  separately ages it — profit overstated, and the same cost at risk of being charged twice.
  Four live schedules did exactly this (3 → 1710 Technology Development, 1 → 2410 Convertible Notes,
  awaiting Gaurav's re-code/cancel ruling). Same root cause as the "journal-born asset" false alarm:
  the registration pass now excludes the engine's own postings (`_SCHEDULED_SOURCES`) so a release
  debiting an asset account can never be registered as a fresh capital purchase. (Gaurav, 2026-08-17)

- **DA-12 OPEN.** (a) ~~Kaveesh confirmation of DA-8 lives~~ LOCKED 2026-08-17 — **Gaurav owns the useful lives**, not Kaveesh; only tax-book divergence (SG capital allowances / AU ATO) stays with Kaveesh at year-close;
  (b) DQ-111 residue ruling (S$468.93 of bounced-TT bank fees sitting inside the 1710 base);
  (c) history-year asset backfill (finalized years bypass approve, so the trigger never fires);
  (d) the DA-6 screen (the Amortization tab is still a placeholder); (e) INSP-10 (any asset-class
  balance with no policy/register coverage is an exception).
